### [1] [The Ising lattice gas and the surface diffusion of CO on Cu(001) at finite coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33147)
*S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将以中间散射函数（ISF）的记忆方程为框架，研究 Kawasaki 动力学下 Ising 格点气体的相干中间散射函数，并将其应用于 CO/Cu(001) 表面扩散，发现测得的退相速率接近关联时间的倒数而非初值，且需要至少 35–50 meV 的最近邻排斥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前针对格点气体的中间散射函数记忆方程虽已推导，但尚未系统应用于 Ising 格点气体；同时表面扩散实验测得的退相速率与静态量（结构和跳变速率）之间的关系尚不明确，需要厘清记忆效应何时重要、何时可以仅由平衡态静态量预测动力学。

Method: 作者将记忆方程应用于 Kawasaki 动力学下的 Ising 格点气体，解析求出一维通道中静态结构因子和有效跳变速率的闭合形式，并借助求和规则导出单指数下界；利用精确弛豫谱在弱耦合与强吸引、排斥层等不同情形下检验该单指数的适用性，并通过拟合近常数的退相速率、归一化衰减下的面积（即关联时间）连接求和规则与实验数据，最终对照 CO/Cu(001) 沿两个晶向的测量。

Result: 单指数（仅由平衡态量构建）只在弱耦合下复现关联时间，此时退相速率可由静态量读出；强吸引下团簇的缓慢溶解使关联时间延长至九倍，而排斥层几乎在其结构峰处如单模弛豫。数值拟合的退相速率趋近最慢弛豫速率而非初值，求和规则仅通过归一化衰减下的面积（关联时间）与数据吻合。对 CO/Cu(001)，格点气体模型能复现沿两个晶向测得的退相速率，且无论跳变速率取值如何都要求至少 35–50 meV 的近邻排斥，测得的速率接近关联时间倒数，并预言记忆效应随覆盖度增大而增强。

Conclusion: 记忆效应显著影响中间散射函数的衰减，静态量单独不足以始终预测关联时间；在 CO/Cu(001) 中，实验退相速率反映的是最慢弛豫（关联时间倒数），并指向 35–50 meV 的近邻排斥，同时记忆效应随覆盖度增加而增强。

Abstract: The memory equation of the coherent intermediate scattering function (ISF), derived previously for lattice gases, is applied to the Ising lattice gas under Kawasaki dynamics. The ISF follows the decay of a density wave of adparticles, a superposition of exponential relaxation modes: the decay starts at a rate fixed by a sum rule and is then slowed by a memory. The static structure factor and the effective hop rate are obtained in closed form, exactly along a one-dimensional channel; with the sum rule they give a single exponential, a lower bound on the ISF. Exact relaxation spectra show that this single exponential, built from equilibrium quantities alone, reproduces the correlation time only at weak coupling; only there can a measured dephasing rate be read through the statics. For strong attraction the slow dissolution of clusters lengthens the correlation time up to ninefold, whereas a repulsive layer relaxes nearly as a single mode at its structure peak. The dephasing rate, fitted over a time window where it is nearly constant, approaches the slowest relaxation rate, not the initial one; the sum rule reaches the data only through the area beneath the normalized decay, which defines the correlation time. For CO/Cu(001), the lattice gas reproduces the dephasing rates measured along two crystal directions and requires a nearest-neighbour repulsion of at least $35$--$50$~meV, whatever the jump rates; the measured rate is then close to the inverse correlation time, and the memory is predicted to grow with coverage.

</details>


### [2] [Optimal Search Strategies under Finite Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33316)
*Hugues Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限资源预算约束下的目标搜索问题，通过在一维扩散搜索者上配置外力、部分反射旗帜和自化学趋化线索三种资源，推导出知情搜索与盲搜索的最优资源配置协议，并分析非马尔可夫线索对搜索性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标搜索是许多自然和人工智能体面临的挑战，通常需要消耗能量或物质资源。现有模型通常假设资源虽昂贵但无限供应，而实际上资源往往只能有限量产生。本文旨在突破无限资源假设，研究在有限资源预算下必须全程合理分配资源的问题。

Method: 作者在一维扩散搜索者上配置三种资源：外力、部分反射旗帜和自化学趋化线索。对于知情搜索（智能体能持续获取过去与当前位置信息），推导出精确的最优资源分配协议；再确定盲搜索者的对应协议。重点考察能产生环境记忆的非马尔可夫线索在有位置信息与无位置信息情况下的作用。

Result: 研究发现：知情搜索的最优资源配置总是在空间上集中；而盲搜索者的资源使用必须在空间上更均匀地分布。当智能体拥有位置信息时，能产生环境记忆的非马尔可夫线索即使在有限资源约束下也能显著提升搜索性能；但在盲搜索中其益处相当有限，尤其是当线索不提供方向信息时。

Conclusion: 有限资源约束显著改变了最优搜索策略的空间特性：知情搜索倾向于集中投放资源，盲搜索则需均匀分配。环境记忆线索的效能高度依赖智能体对位置信息的获取程度，提示在资源受限场景下，搜索策略的设计需兼顾信息可及性与资源分配方式。

Abstract: Improving target search is a challenge faced by many natural and artificial agents, often requiring the use of energetic or material resources. These inputs may be supplied by an external controller or generated by the searcher itself, for example, through the deposition of cues along its trajectory. Although such resources are commonly modeled as costly but available without an explicit limit, in practice, they can often only be produced in finite amounts. Here, we go beyond the assumption of an unlimited supply of resources by investigating searches in which a finite resource budget must be allocated throughout the search process. To investigate this problem, we equip a one-dimensional diffusive searcher with three types of resources: an external force, a supply of partially reflecting flags, and auto-chemotactic cues. We derive exact optimal resource-allocation protocols for informed search processes in which the agent has continuous access to its past and current positions, which we find to always require resource allocation to be spatially concentrated. We then determine the corresponding protocols for blind searchers and show that resource usage must be more evenly distributed. Non-Markovian cues that generate environmental memory are found to substantially improve search performance when the agent has access to positional information, even under finite-resource constraints. In contrast, their benefits are considerably more limited for blind search, particularly when the cues provide no directional information.

</details>


### [3] [Distributional statistics of nonequilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33383)
*Harrison Hartle,Aanjaneya Kumar,David H. Wolpert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个参考建模框架，用于描述受约束的随机概率分布。框架将 Crooks 的超系综方法推广为在分类分布单位单纯形上构造最大不确定性的 Jaynes 先验，重点研究平均能量与 Shannon 熵约束下的模型，并展示了其在非平衡建模中的解析、采样与贝叶斯推断应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束条件下的随机概率分布缺乏统一的参考建模框架。传统 Jaynes 最大熵方法只给出唯一平衡分布，难以刻画远离平衡的非平衡状态；作者希望建立一种"最大不确定性"的参考模型，使平均能量等物理约束仅确定单纯形上的一个超平面子集，从而能在该集合内对所有可能分布进行均匀采样，以描述一般性的非平衡特性。

Method: 将 Crooks 的超系综方法推广，在分类分布的单位单纯形上构造 Jaynesian 最大不确定性先验。对平均能量的精确约束唯一确定 Boltzmann-Gibbs 平衡态，同时定义单纯形中所有具有该平均能量值的分布的"超平面子集"，并赋予该子集均匀概率密度以构成超系综。进一步引入 Shannon 熵约束，实现对非平衡程度可调（tunable）的参数化访问。方法支持解析计算与精确采样算法，并与标准贝叶斯推断兼容。

Result: 该框架可解析地预测随机分布的平均微态分布与模态微态分布、随机分布的期望自由能等一般性非平衡性质。当 Shannon 熵最大化时框架约化为 Boltzmann-Gibbs 统计。在三个场景中验证了其兼容性与实用性：(i) 与超统计（superstatistics）准平衡建模的比较；(ii) 随机非平衡稳态的生成；(iii) 以先验超系综进行贝叶斯推断。

Conclusion: 所提出的参考建模框架具有普适性，遵循传统 Jaynesian 建模逻辑，与标准贝叶斯推断及多种非平衡建模方法广泛兼容，并在熵最大化时回归 Boltzmann-Gibbs 统计。作者展望了其在理论扩展与实证应用方面的诸多前景。

Abstract: We establish a reference modeling framework for random probability distributions subject to constraints. Generalizing the hyperensemble approach of Crooks, we formulate Jaynesian priors of maximal uncertainty over the unit simplex of categorical distributions. This framework is entirely general, but we primarily investigate models with constraints on physically relevant quantities, namely, average energy and Shannon entropy. A constraint on the exact value of average energy uniquely determines a Boltzmann-Gibbs equilibrium, but also defines a hyperplanar subset of the probability simplex consisting of all distributions with that value of average energy. The associated hyperensemble has a uniform probability density across all such distributions, regardless of distance to equilibrium. Such reference models make predictions of generic nonequilibrium properties such as the mean and modal microstate distributions and the expected free energy of a random distribution, which we examine analytically and via exact sampling algorithms. Moreover, introducing constraints on Shannon entropy permits parametric access to tunably nonequilibrium regimes. The approach follows conventional Jaynesian modeling logic and is compatible with standard Bayesian inference, and reduces to Boltzmann-Gibbs statistics when Shannon entropy is maximized. We examine the physical predictions of this scheme, and demonstrate its wide compatibility with other approaches to nonequilibrium modeling. We illustrate the framework in three scenarios: (i) comparison to superstatistics quasi-equilibrium modeling, (ii) generation of random nonequilibrium steady states, and (iii) Bayesian inference under a prior hyperensemble. We highlight numerous prospects for theoretical extensions and empirical application.

</details>


### [4] [Large spin large deviations for interacting particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33523)
*Cristian Giardinà,Tomohiro Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种大自旋极限（s→∞）下直接在格点上分析相互作用粒子系统样本路径大偏差的新框架，通过变量尺度变换使过程聚集于由格点扩散方程支配的确定性轨迹，并从尺度化微观生成元显式计算出路径空间大偏差速率函数（基于Feng–Kurtz理论），速率函数由格点上以经典自旋变量描述的哈密顿量给出。主模型对称调和过程对应的哈密顿量与经典不可积非紧Ishimori自旋链相同。该理论与宏观涨落理论（MFT）一致，并在空间连续极限下平滑回归标准MFT哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宏观涨落理论（MFT）在空间连续极限下存在空间紫外奇异性与可能的连续密度爆破问题，需要一种天然正则化的格点版本MFT，能够避免这些病态行为，同时建立从微观动力学到宏观大偏差的贯通联系。

Method: 核心方法包括：1）引入大自旋极限（s→∞）并对变量做适当尺度变换；2）从尺度化微观生成元出发，基于Feng–Kurtz理论显式计算路径空间大偏差速率函数；3）得到格点上以经典自旋变量表述的哈密顿量；4）在空间连续极限下与标准MFT哈密顿量对照验证一致性；5）进行泊松噪声Martin–Siggia–Rose（MSR）计算，直接从微观动力学推导非线性格点哈密顿量；6）用相对熵论证获得密度-流作用量；7）建立格点涨落定理及其对称性起源；8）将该方案应用于部分对称排斥过程、对称包含过程、布朗能量过程及可积热传导模型。

Result: 主模型对称调和过程的大偏差哈密顿量被识别为经典不可积非紧Ishimori自旋链；大自旋大偏差理论与传统MFT完全一致，并在空间连续极限下平滑恢复标准MFT哈密顿量；所提框架提供了天然正则化的格点版MFT，规避空间紫外奇异和连续密度爆破；对多个典型过程的计算显示其大偏差哈密顿量与经典紧致和非紧致自旋链相联系。

Conclusion: 该框架为大偏差理论提供了一个正则化的格点类比，成功连接微观动力学与宏观涨落理论，并通过与经典自旋链的哈密顿量对应关系揭示了大偏差结构与可积系统的深层联系，为分析非平衡多体系统异常涨落提供了新工具。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for analyzing the sample-path large deviations of interacting particle systems directly on a lattice by considering a large-spin limit ($s \to \infty$). Through an appropriate scaling of variables, the process concentrates around its deterministic trajectory, governed by the lattice diffusion equation. We quantify the exponential cost of observing atypical trajectories by explicitly computing the path-space large deviation rate function from the scaled microscopic generator, based on a theory due to Feng and Kurtz. The rate function is described in terms of a Hamiltonian on a lattice in terms of classical spin variables. Notably, for our main model, the symmetric harmonic process, the corresponding Hamiltonian is identified with the classical integrable non-compact Ishimori spin chain.
  Furthermore, we show that our large spin large deviation theory is fully consistent with traditional Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (MFT), smoothly recovering the standard MFT Hamiltonian in the spatial continuous limit. We also perform a Poisson-noise Martin--Siggia--Rose (MSR) computation to derive the non-linear lattice Hamiltonian directly from microscopic dynamics, use a relative entropy argument to obtain the density-current action, and establish the lattice fluctuation theorem alongside its symmetry origin. Overall, our framework provides a naturally regularized, lattice analogue of MFT that circumvents spatial ultraviolet singularities and possible continuous density blow-ups.
  We also apply our new scheme to partial symmetric exclusion process, symmetric inclusion processes, Brownian energy process and integrable heat conduction model, showing how their large-deviation Hamiltonians relate to classical compact and non-compact spin chains.

</details>


### [5] [Jensen-Mercer two-sided bounds for entropy production rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34112)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用凸性结构，在连续随机系统的熵产生率估计中，从Jensen下界出发推导出由Mercer反射不等式启发的互补上界，形成对总熵产生与子系统熵产生的双边夹逼，并拓展到位置依赖扩散、欠阻尼动力学和倾斜周期势等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵产生将有限观测转化为对隐藏耗散的约束。传统Jensen下界仅通过平均坐标速率给出熵产生率的下界，缺乏对应的上界，难以完整界定熵产生的范围。作者希望利用相同的凸性结构构造互补上界，从而仅凭平均漂移与局域流的极值即可实现对熵产生的双边约束。

Method: 针对过阻尼Langevin动力学的二次熵产生泛函，证明Mercer反射不等式等价于尖锐的Bhatia-Davis方差界，并等于局域流范围内该泛函最小仿射主控函数的凸对偶。由此将下界与上界组织为最优界层级，每次新增观测都收紧上界。同时热力学不确定性关系提供独立的涨落下限。进一步处理位置依赖扩散、欠阻尼动力学和倾斜周期势，其中弱势垒级数得到闭式解、强势垒区用渐近方法求解，并对布朗马达进行了数值检验。

Result: 建立了仅由平均漂移和局域流极值控制的熵产生双边夹逼界，形成一个层次化的最优界体系；热力学不确定性关系给出独立的下限；倾斜周期势的弱势垒级数为闭式、强势垒区渐近求解，并数值验证于布朗马达。

Conclusion: 相同的凸性结构同时产生Jensen下界与Mercer反射上界，实现对熵产生率的双边夹逼，且该上界与Bhatia-Davis方差界及凸对偶结构一致；这一框架可推广到多种动力学情形，并在数值上得到验证。

Abstract: Bounds on entropy production convert limited observations into constraints on hidden dissipation. The Jensen bound expresses a lower bound on the total and subsystem entropy production rates of continuous stochastic systems in terms of mean coordinate rates alone. We show that the same convexity structure yields a complementary upper bound, inspired by Mercer's reflection inequality. For the quadratic entropy-production functional of overdamped Langevin dynamics, Mercer's reflection inequality is identical to the sharp Bhatia-Davis variance bound and coincides with the convex dual of the smallest affine majorant of the functional over the range of the local current. The two bounds yield a two-sided sandwich controlled solely by the mean drift and the extrema of the current, organized into a hierarchy of optimal bounds in which each further observation tightens the ceiling. The thermodynamic uncertainty relation supplies an independent fluctuation floor. We also treat position-dependent diffusion, underdamped dynamics and the tilted periodic potential, for which the weak-barrier series is obtained in closed form and the strong-barrier regime is solved asymptotically. We check the bounds numerically on the Brownian motor.

</details>


### [6] [Learning by Confusion Locates a Parametrization-Dependent Fisher Information Maximum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34769)
*George Issa,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从理论上阐明"困惑学习"(LBC)定位相变点的机制：推导出贝叶斯最优困惑峰的精确平衡条件，证明对单扫参的平衡系统，该峰紧贴费舍尔信息的最大值，从而给出其有限尺寸标度，并揭示其位置依赖于训练数据的参数化方式（如T、lnT或β网格），在多种模型上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LBC作为一种免序参量的机器学习定位相变方法，其特征精度曲线的中心峰被视为相变点，但该峰的位置及其有限尺寸标度由什么决定一直不清楚，缺乏理论依据。

Method: 作者推导了贝叶斯最优困惑峰的精确平衡条件，并将其与费舍尔信息联系起来；在Ising、Blume-Capel、Potts和Baxter-Wu等模型上，对连续、三临界和一阶相变进行数值检验，同时比较了在T、lnT、β=1/T均匀网格上采样对有限尺寸赝临界轨迹的影响。

Result: 证明困惑峰紧跟费舍尔信息的最大值，从而直接给出其有限尺寸标度；发现同一批位形若按T、lnT或β均匀网格排列，会得到不同的有限尺寸赝临界轨迹，理论预测在多种模型和相变类型上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究确认了LBC实际测量的统计量即费舍尔信息，并表明基于有限尺寸的机器学习相变估计不仅取决于物理本质，还取决于训练数据的参数化方式。

Abstract: Learning by confusion (LBC) is a machine-learning method for locating phase transitions without assuming a known order parameter. Its characteristic accuracy curve contains a central maximum that is interpreted as the transition point, but what determines the location and finite-size scaling of this maximum has remained unclear. We derive an exact balance condition for the Bayes-optimal confusion peak and show that, for equilibrium systems with a single swept parameter, the peak closely follows the maximum of the corresponding Fisher information. This immediately gives its finite-size scaling and reveals an unexpected dependence on how the data are sampled: the same configurations arranged on grids uniform in $T$, $\ln T$, or $β=1/T$ can yield different finite-size pseudocritical trajectories. Tests on the Ising, Blume-Capel, Potts, and Baxter-Wu models confirm the prediction across continuous, tricritical, and first-order transitions. Our results identify the statistical quantity measured by LBC and show that finite-size machine-learning estimates can depend on the parametrization of the training data as well as on the underlying physics.

</details>


### [7] [Extremal and global-time statistics of sluggish random walks with stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34568)
*Theophilus Dwamena Frimpong*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维“惰性随机游走”（扩散率随距原点距离呈幂律衰减）在泊松重置下的极值与全局时间统计量，通过变量变换映射为带符号Bessel过程，并运用更新理论得到有限时间窗内最大值的精确拉普拉斯变换，进而给出长时间渐近行为及若干时间统计量的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the extremal and global-time statistics of a one-dimensional sluggish random walk, whose diffusivity decays as a power law of the distance from the origin, under Poissonian resetting to the origin. A change of variable maps the process onto a signed Bessel process, so that renewal theory applies. We obtain the exact Laplace transform of the distribution of the maximum over a finite window. At long times the centered maximum of the transformed process follows the Gumbel law, and in physical space the typical maximum grows as a power of the logarithm of the window length. The far-tail rate function does not depend on $α$ in the Bessel variable, so the second-order dynamical transition known for Brownian resetting, between maxima produced by a single anomalously long inter-reset interval and maxima produced by an interval occupying essentially the whole window, holds for every $α$. The time of the maximum is uniformly distributed in the bulk of the window, with boundary layers that shift its mean above the midpoint logarithmically in the window length. The last-passage time through the origin has an exact density, and the time elapsed since it converges to a Gamma law. Reflection symmetry makes the occupation time of the positive half-line and the last-passage time uncorrelated. The results reduce to the Brownian case and agree with simulations.

A one-percent change of lattice spacing alters the bulk stiffness by approximately 5--9\%, comparable to the boundary contribution. For the same defective state, two bulk references give relative excesses of 0.027\% and 4.15\%. Matching the comparator to grain strain and chemical potential yields positive diagonal excesses in paired preparations at three interaction strengths. Within a twelve-state family with comparable grain interiors, the excess is robust to 288 bulk substitutions and follows an approximate length-per-area relation. Independent fixed-area pairs yield a line-response coefficient 3.1\% higher than the three-size estimate. Size and directional dependence restrict this relation to the specified crystal conditions. Reduced link networks relate the directional response to the organization of boundary connections but do not accurately resolve every small bulk-subtracted excess. The results distinguish boundary enhancement from changes in the surrounding crystal and establish a quantitative comparison protocol for superfluid transport in defective crystals.

Abstract: We present a physical explaination of the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) for the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model, which identifies the decomposition of the cavity variables into layers with the random overlap structure of Aizenman--Sims--Starr. We describe the low-temperature excitations through a dilute gas of defects relative to the ground state. This mechanism is developed first with a finite number of defects and then extended to exponential populations of states. Result: 得到标度关系 ⟨x_∥(t)⟩ ∝ F_∥^α t^α Ω^{-(1-α)}，其中F_∥为有效纵向力，Ω为横向截面格点数。表明通道越窄（Ω越小），异常迁移率越强。解析结果适用于任意d维Bravais晶格且横向为周期边界；数值模拟在不同晶格几何下确认了解析预测，即便采用反射边界也定量吻合。

Conclusion: 几何约束通过缩小通道截面显著增强玻璃态介质中的异常迁移率，标度为 Ω^{-(1-α)}。该结果为理解受限空间中的异常输运提供了定量理论，且对边界条件类型具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of geometrical confinement on driven transport in anomalous media within the framework of the quenched trap model. The particle performs a biased random walk on a lattice, where quenched disorder introduces power-law distributed trapping times. We consider channels that are infinite along one direction and have a finite cross-section in the remaining $d-1$ directions. In the glassy regime, where $α=T/T_g<1$, we derive an analytical expression for the mean longitudinal displacement in the long-time and weak-force limits. We find the scaling $\langle x_\parallel(t)\rangle \propto F_\parallel^αt^αΩ^{-(1-α)}$, where $F_\parallel>0$ is the effective longitudinal force and $Ω$ is the number of sites in a transverse cross-section. Thus, the anomalous mobility is enhanced for narrower channels. Our analytical result applies to general Bravais lattices in $d$ dimensions with periodic transverse boundary conditions. Numerical simulations confirm the analytical predictions for different lattice geometries and show quantitative agreement even for channels with reflecting boundary conditions.

</details>

Abstract: While classical thin-film hydrodynamics models widely govern macroscopic fluid layers, they fail to capture the molecular-level thermal fluctuations that dictate nanoscale interfacial dynamics. Standard stochastic extensions rely on the long-wave approximation of the continuum fluctuating Navier-Stokes equations; however, this top-down paradigm lacks a formal connection to microscopic particle trajectories and cannot readily accommodate complex, non-equilibrium boundary mechanisms. We resolve this limitation by deriving the stochastic thin-film equation directly from the exact underlying N-particle Hamiltonian dynamics. This is achieved via a systematic, bottom-up coarse-graining framework based on a generalized fluctuating dynamic density-functional theory. Crucially, this first principles formulation offers unparalleled flexibility, enabling the seamless integration of multi-layered interfaces and non-conserved dynamics such as evaporation. For these complex configurations, our framework rigorously yields entirely novel stochastic thin-film equations featuring emergent noise terms that encapsulate microscopic fluctuations missing from classical top-down models.

</details>

  not collapse onto a single curve against an effective temperature, and that they
  depend strongly on the detection threshold.

Abstract: Quantum private broadcasting (\textsf{QPB}) asks whether a quantum message can be encrypted once, broadcast to several recipients, and decrypted locally while the joint ciphertext reveals no plaintext information. Abstract: In this work, we present a fractional framework for reversible two-state chemical kinetics derived from the Continuous-Time Random Walk (CTRW) formalism with Mittag-Leffler waiting-time distributions. An exact analytical solution is obtained, yielding closed-form expressions for the time evolution of the state populations. To the best of our knowledge, a time-driven Monte Carlo algorithm is developed for the first time to simulate the irreversible underlying non-Markovian stochastic dynamics governed by a system of Caputo fractional differential equations. The proposed framework is applied to the conformational dynamics of the TbQSOX enzyme, whose relaxation exhibits power-law behavior. Abstract: We consider a classical Coulomb gas of $N$ particles in dimension $d\geq2$, confined by a general external potential $U$ in the large-$N$ limit. In that limit the density of particles has a compact support called the droplet. We study smooth linear statistics ${\mathcal L}_N=\sum_{i=1}^N f({\bf x}_i)$ at large $N$. While the leading variance of ${\mathcal L}_N$ is by now well understood in many settings, much less is known about higher cumulants. When $f({\bf x})=f(|{\bf x}|)$ and the potential is rotationally invariant, these cumulants were computed recently at leading order. We derive explicit formulas for the leading third and fourth cumulants for general smooth droplets, without assuming that either the potential or the test function is radial. The answers are expressed as intrinsic boundary integrals over the equilibrium droplet. The third cumulant depends on the cube of the jump of the normal derivative of the harmonic extension of $f$, while the fourth cumulant also involves the exterior Dirichlet-to-Neumann operator, the mean curvature of the boundary, and normal derivatives of the background density. Our derivation uses the tilted potential $U_s=U+s f/β$, the associated deformation of the droplet, and shape derivatives of the Dirichlet energy. This deformation has a natural interpretation as the infinitesimal motion of the free boundary in the obstacle-problem formulation of the equilibrium measure. Our formulas extend previous studies by Wiegmann and Zabrodin in two dimensions for $β=2$. In their setting, we provide a rigorous proof of the limiting third and fourth cumulants, supporting the proposed formulas for general Coulomb gases. We specialize the general formulas to the disk, the half-plane and the sphere, and check them against exactly solvable non-radial deformations, including translated elliptic droplets in $d=2$ and spheroidal deformations in $d=3$.

Abstract: We study the global $V$-representability problem in time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT) for the two-particle Hubbard dimer model in the spin-singlet sector. Lett. 136 (2026) 256401, Phys. Rev. A 113 (2026) 062222). For both principles, we prove global-in-time $V$-representability, show that singular times induce non-uniqueness of the time-dependent effective potential, and classify the set of solutions.

Abstract: The triangle map is an area-preserving, piecewise-parabolic map of the torus with vanishing Lyapunov exponent. We study the symbolic process $X_n=\pm1$ given by the two halves of the torus, whose partial sums $S_n$ are the transport of the lifted momentum, with an integrator free of accumulated rounding errors and exact computations on polygonal cylinder sets. We prove that the Koopman operator has no non-constant $H^1$ eigenfunctions, that the symbol is not an $H^1$ coboundary, and that runs of equal symbols are uniformly bounded. For generic parameters the walk is normally diffusive and Gaussian, but its diffusion coefficient is not that of a coin: away from the edges of the parameter square it varies between $0.2$ and $14$ times the Bernoulli value, and for a benchmark pair, with $D=3.09\pm0.01$, the excess is produced by the order of the bounded inter-event intervals, not by their distribution. The first-passage exponent is $1/2$ with a non-Bernoulli amplitude, and worst-case Birkhoff averages decay as for a uniquely ergodic system. For $β=0$ the Green-Kubo sum cancels, $D=0$, yet $\mathrm{Var}(S_n)$ keeps growing slowly, sign changes are rare and the correlations of Fourier modes decay as $n^{-0.2}$. We identify the zero-sum words, which become pure twists at $β=0$, as resonances that trap orbits with a computable $1/\ell$ law, set the crossover time $β^{-1/2}$ at small $β$, and, in an asymmetric deformation of the symbol, produce a logarithmic term in the variance whose coefficient we compute exactly. We formulate conjectures on unique ergodicity, mixing, the central limit theorem and the cohomology of the symbol.

Abstract: Long-range correlations in written texts extend over scales far beyond local syntax, but their relation to semantic organisation remains incompletely understood. Previous work has linked these correlations to the bursty recurrence of topically relevant words. Here we generalise that construction from individual lexical items to distributed semantic observables. We map a text token by token onto a trajectory of pre-trained word embeddings, use principal component analysis (PCA) to identify text-specific directions of semantic variation, and threshold the projected series to define inter-event times; their coefficients of variation form a component-resolved burstiness spectrum $\{B_k\}$. Across 28 literary and scientific texts and the first 20 components, the originals remain systematically above the geometric inter-event-time baseline and above two complementary controls: a word-shuffled null, which preserves the embedding covariance, the PCA eigensystem and the marginal projected distributions while destroying temporal order, and a fractional-Gaussian-noise surrogate, which preserves the Zipfian frequency structure and matches the detrended fluctuation analysis (DFA) exponent of the original rank sequence. With events defined by the upper 5% tail of each projection, the original exceeds both controls in every text-component pair, while the two controls are statistically indistinguishable from each other. Autocorrelation and detrended fluctuation analysis of the semantic projections separate the originals from both controls in the same way. Global rank-level long-range correlations are therefore insufficient to generate the bursty recurrence observed along semantic directions. The results instead support an interpretation in terms of the ordered recurrence of semantically related material.

</details>

Math., 23(1) by Lieb and Simon. To demonstrate the potential and the limits of our method, we also compute explicit lower bounds for the ground-state energies $E(N,N)$ of all atoms with $2 \leq N \leq 86$ and we compare them with the bounds obtained in J. Chem. Phys., 62(8) by Hertel, Lieb, Thirring and the bounds obtained by Seco in his dissertation. Counterexamples show the essential sharpness of both assumptions.

The exponent depends on the step, while the constant is uniform in steps, frequency and energy on compact intervals. For every irrational frequency and every energy with positive Lyapunov exponent, we prove non-atomicity of both Oseledets direction distributions and convergence to the unstable distribution from every phase-independent initial probability. This gives a new criterion for a zero Lyapunov exponent: a subsequential limit with an atom for one fixed initial direction suffices. All these results extend to an analytic class, with step-dependent regularity bounds.

Abstract: Classical shell fracture theories predominantly consider cracks aligned with principal curvature directions in cylindrical and spherical shells under tensile mode or pressure loading, leaving the fracture behavior of arbitrarily oriented cracks in doubly curved shells under general loading poorly understood. Here, we introduce a semi-analytical framework based on Reissner shallow-shell theory to investigate isotropic shells with positive, zero, and negative Gaussian curvature. The formulation computes stress intensity factors, energy release rates, and crack-lip displacements for arbitrarily inclined through-thickness cracks in spheres, ellipsoids, cylinders, hyperboloids, and minimal surfaces under general mechanical loading. We show that shell curvature couples opening, sliding, and tearing, allowing a single loading component to activate multiple fracture modes, unlike in flat plates. This coupling depends strongly on crack orientation and the normal, parallel, and torsional components of the shell curvature. Ellipsoids exhibit weak coupling, hyperboloids generally exhibit the strongest coupling, and cylinders display the most complex orientation-dependent responses. Moreover, in-plane shear can induce crack closure in hyperboloidal shells, suggesting a potential geometric mechanism for enhanced damage resistance. These findings elucidate the role of local curvature in fracture-mode coupling and provide a mechanistic foundation for the analysis of shellular (meta-)structures with engineered curvature distributions.

Abstract: The layer Hall effect provides a prototypical example of a hidden layer response in van der Waals bilayers: opposite layers carry compensating Hall currents at zero out-of-plane electric field, while an applied out-of-plane electric field $E_z$ selects one layer over the other and switches the net Hall response. Tensor components that are odd under this operation are forced to cancel between layers at $E_z=0$, and acquire a macroscopic response satisfying $T_α(+E_z)=-T_α(-E_z)$. This criterion allows us to enumerate the symmetry-allowed electrically reversible tensor components associated with different Jahn symbols and to identify their physical realizations in common response tensors. Model calculations and first-principles simulations further confirm the predicted field-odd responses. Abstract: Magnon-phonon hybridization provides a powerful route to couple spin and lattice excitations, yet the symmetry principle governing mode selectivity remains unclear. Here, we show that crystalline pseudoangular momentum provides a general criterion for selective magnon-phonon hybridization in noncollinear multisublattice magnetic systems. An out-of-plane magnetic field tunes one magnon sector into resonance with a degenerate phonon doublet, yet only the pseudoangular-momentum-matched phonon develops a pronounced avoided crossing while the other remains nearly unmixed. Field reversal switches the selected rotation sector and reverses the rotational polarization of the hybridized phonon component without changing the hybrid-mode energies. Abstract: Nonlinear electrical transport enables the conversion and detection of electromagnetic signals, supporting wireless energy harvesting, sensing and frequency conversion. Achieving both a large response and electrical tunability remains a central materials challenge. Here we harness giant third-order nonlinearity to amplify and electrically control second-harmonic transport in the correlated polar metal Ca3Ru2O7. Superposing a dc bias on an ac excitation enhances the second-harmonic signal by more than an order of magnitude. The bias controls the polarity of the induced response and activates channels forbidden by symmetry at zero bias. Both transverse and longitudinal components scale quadratically with the ac excitation and are odd in the dc bias, with angular dependences consistent with third-order conductivity. The underlying third-harmonic electric-field responsivity reaches 4.5 \times 10-6 m2V-2 at 3 K, providing a quantitative measure of the strong nonlinearity. The induced response emerges below the spin-reorientation transition near 48 K, linking its enhancement to the reconstructed low-temperature electronic state. Theoretical calculations identify quantum-geometric contributions to the nonlinear conductivity and reproduce the observed response symmetry. These results demonstrate how strong higher-order nonlinearity can amplify an effective second-order response, providing a route to electrically tunable nonlinear transport in correlated materials.

Abstract: We propose a uniform construction of affine schemes encoding the Higgs branches and Macdonald indices of the Argyres--Douglas theories $\mathcal{D}_p(ADE)$, with $\gcd(p,h^\vee)=1$. The construction captures conjectural OPE decoupling relations via the Jacobian ring of a single invariant polynomial potential, obtained from a polynomial twisted period of the corresponding Coxeter orbit space. We show that these potentials are ``syzygy-maximizing'' in the first jet schemes and recover the Higgs branches as their reduced critical loci. We obtain closed determinant expressions for the classical types and explicit potentials for $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$. Powers of the nilradical define an intrinsic filtration on the resulting Jacobian rings, from which we compute candidate Macdonald indices with the correct Schur and Hall--Littlewood limits, in the examples studied. Restricting the potentials to Slodowy slices extends the prescription to nilpotently Higgsed theories. Abstract: We present novel graph-centered algorithms for the construction and representation of worldline effective field theory (WEFT) integrands that separate the topological and algebraic structure of loop integrands into independent graph-numerator pairs. In this representation, the graph encodes all propagators, worldline source and sink vertices, momentum routing, i0 prescriptions, and proper-time dependence, while the numerator carries only the minimal algebraic, tensorial content. To construct integrands at fixed post-Minkowskian order we propose a generalized Berends-Giele construction that minimizes the number of expensive tensor contraction operations. We also discuss simplification and canonicalization strategies through "serialization" of worldline propagators and the application of "selfie"-subdiagram partial-fraction identities, yielding a minimal canonical topology basis. We further show that the graph-numerator representation naturally generalizes the notion of an integral family and its sectors, relevant for integration-by-parts (IBP) reduction: propagators are not constrained to a single master labeling, and each sector carries its own minimal set of independent scalar products, completing into an overcomplete global family. This setup minimizes the total number of required integral sectors through an optimal use of sector symmetries and enables independent optimization of propagator labeling for IBP reductions. The algorithms are implemented in the Rust crate FeynRIR, which is currently able to efficiently compute integrands for the variation of the effective WEFT action, the equations of motion for the worldlines and the (radiation-reacted) impulse.

</details>

Abstract: We explore effective field theory (EFT) realizations of the dark dimension (DD) scenario in which the size of the DD is determined by the competition between a 5D quintessence potential and the Casimir energy. When only contributions from massless fields are considered, the Casimir energy does not have a typical energy scale, and the 4D dark energy (DE) scale is set by the height of the 5D quintessence potential. The simple 5D model we first study has an attractive feature that the size of the DD is related to the 4D vacuum energy (VE), as outlined above. However, it turns out that the 4D VE thus obtained cannot work as the 4D DE in the standard cosmological setting. One of the discrepancies between the VE and the DE manifests in the radion dependence of the effective 4D Newton's gravitational constant. This leads us to explore a 5D model in which the 4D Einstein-Hilbert (EH) action is dominantly induced by quantum effects, leaving the radion dependence of the 4D Newton's constant subdominant. This model leads to an attractive cosmological model, realizing the mechanism explained in the beginning. We also explore a 6D model with the total internal volume fixed, to make the 4D Newton's constant independent of the radion. We first study a scenario in which the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions are of the same order. In this scenario, the physical origin of the size of the DD is left to its UV completion. Next, we turn to a scenario in which there is a large hierarchy between the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions. In this scenario, the energy scale of the 5D quintessence potential turns out to be much higher than the energy scale of the 4D DE as inferred from the cosmological observations, so we need to subtract a constant term in 4D to tune the 4D DE. This prevents us from obtaining the scaling relation for the DE expected in the dSDC.

Conclusion: 辅助场形式为非线性电动力学的 TTbar 型流提供了简洁且可精确求解的框架：无关变形是势的重标定、边缘变形是真空平移，核 F(r) 统一编码了因果性、自对偶性、特征锥与能量条件等物理约束，并为多类具体模型给出了精确流解。

Abstract: Causal self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics admits a simple auxiliary-field description: the Lagrangian is linear in the two Lorentz invariants of the field strength, and the dynamics is encoded in a potential $W(\varphi)$ of a single auxiliary variable. We study non-perturbative $T\bar T$-like flows of these theories in this formalism. The irrelevant (stress-tensor) deformation acts on the potential by a rescaling, while the marginal ModMax-type deformation translates its vacuum. We derive the resulting flow equations exactly, show that the irrelevant exact $T\bar T$-like flow takes the autonomous form $\partial_λ\mathcal L=X\,F(r)$, where the closed form kernel $F$ depends only on the ratio $r=-T^μ_μ/\sqrt{4X-Y}$ of stress-tensor invariants. We also give an explicit reconstruction of the auxiliary potential from a given kernel. Causality imposes the differential inequality $D(r)>1/(1-r)$, while a globally single-valued kernel requires the ratio $r$ to be monotonic along the auxiliary branch. We examine the relation between the evenness of the exact $T\bar T$-like flow , the $\varphi$-parity of the potential, and the analyticity of the Lagrangian in the electromagnetic invariants, including the effect of a marginal ModMax parameter. We construct the linear auxiliary and Hamiltonian formulations and show that the Hamiltonian flow is governed by the same exact $T\bar T$-like flow with the opposite sign. We also express Born's Legendre self-duality, the two characteristic cones, and the energy conditions in terms of the kernel. In particular, $r=(\varepsilon-p_\perp)/(\varepsilon+p_\perp)$ has a direct physical interpretation. Exact $T\bar T$-like flow s are obtained for generalized Born-Infeld, logarithmic, q-deformed, and no-$τ$-maximum electrodynamics.

</details>

This localized term inherits the gravitational Weyl anomaly of the smooth ALE resolution, which concentrates under the singular degeneration, along with an additional unprotected heat-kernel coefficient.

Abstract: We establish the existence of a new electromagnetic memory effect in all odd spacetime dimensions. We provide a unified derivation of this effect in all higher dimensions by evaluating the Maxwell constraint on a null plane at large, finite distance. We observe that in odd spacetime dimensions, the lightray integral computing the memory effect does not commute with the large-$r$ expansion. Our results necessarily assume that the lightray integral is taken prior to the $r \rightarrow \infty$ limit. The effect vanishes in the opposite order of limits, in agreement with previous results. We compute the Coulombic order electromagnetic memory effect for both massless and massive sources in all higher dimensions, explain its relation to the Fourier transform of the leading soft photon theorem and present a protocol for its measurement in a finite time parametrically larger than the distance of a detector from the sources. We conclude with comments on the generalization to gravity.

Abstract: This dissertation expounds the biverse volume-normalized gravity, a novel theoretical framework of classical and quantum gravity that provides an unusual solution to the cosmological constant problem, the 60-degree-of-magnitude discrepancy between the value of the vacuum energy density in our universe predicted by particle physics and that implied by cosmological data. In the literature, there exist two interesting ideas: 1) to use the "volume-normalized" action functional, the usual action of the particle Standard Model and general relativity but divided by the spacetime volume, first proposed by Tseytlin; 2) to generate the vacuum energy density dynamically by assigning a ghost-free massive potential to the graviton. However, each has a problem: Tseytlin's attempt suffers from diverging quantum loops and the massive gravity theory has a second metric tensor with questionable origin and the quantum strong coupling problem. Inspired by these two ideas, as well as the AdS/CFT correspondence in string theory, the biverse volume-normalized gravity extends Tseytlin's idea by placing our (3+1)-dimensional universe on the boundary of a (4+1)-dimensional bulk space. This new adaptation controls the large quantum gravity loops in the limit of vanishing ratio of the spacetime volume of our universe to that of the other universe. The ghost-free massive gravity can then be seamlessly incorporated, not only that its otherwise nebulous second metric tensor appears naturally as the pullback of the bulk metric on the boundary, but its strong coupling problem also solved. The biverse volume-normalized gravity is also consistent with all tested models of particle cosmology and the evolution of the universe, and nontrivially predicts that the tensor-to-scalar ratio of our universe is exactly zero.

Abstract: Higher-dimensional theories distinguish the dimensionality of a microscopic description from the number of directions accessible to a physical observer. Relating these notions requires not only dimensional reduction, but also a consistent preservation of operational independence across different levels of description.
  We establish sufficient conditions under which a compositional operational rank is preserved first as the dimension of a smooth integral leaf and then as an observable Fisher-information rank. The key requirement is closure-faithfulness: the smooth realization must preserve the dependency relations of the operational closure, rather than merely its total rank. Together with real representability, constant rank, involutivity, and observer/scale compatibility, this yields a local integral leaf whose dimension equals the operational rank.
  A further primary-identification condition is required at the measurement level. If an independently specified observer image coincides locally with the primary integral distribution and the admissible protocols provide local resolution, finite labeled-mixture closure, and locally constant outcome support, then the same rank can be realized by a finite composite Fisher-information protocol.
  The construction holds for arbitrary finite rank. At r = 4 it provides a conditional operational criterion for dimensional reduction in higher-dimensional theories: a viable reduction must preserve the relevant operational dependency structure, not merely produce four effective coordinates. Quantum-hardware negative controls further show that the same restricted Fisher rank can conceal different larger ranks under a broader pre-specified measurement protocol. The framework therefore constrains the operational outcome of dimensional reduction without imposing a universal four-dimensional bound on microscopic state spaces.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a quantum-field-theoretical framework on the tangent bundle in which gravitational and internal gauge structures are associated with different sectors of an affine tangent-fiber geometry. The translational sector supplies the coframe and its relation to teleparallel gravity. A reduced vertical spectral problem is based on the centrally extended Euclidean group \(E^{c}(2)\), motivated by projective representations of the stabilizer of a nonzero null vector. Twofold multiplicities of equivalent vertical representations in selected left-chiral sectors admit weak \(SU(2)\) actions, while an assumed threefold quark multiplicity admits a locally gauged color \(SU(3)_c\) symmetry. A proposed global quotient correlates the color center with vertical rotations on a threefold rotation cover. Together with the selected chiral spectrum and anomaly conditions, this construction reproduces the observed fractional quark charge assignments. We formulate matter and gauge-field quantization, BRST symmetry, and anomaly constraints. Under the stated projection assumptions, the color sector has the standard local QCD structure, including its one-loop beta function and asymptotic freedom. Additional gauge bosons and dark-matter states are proposed as hypothetical sectors. We also formulate a structural action for possible couplings between teleparallel torsion and additional Lorentz-sector potentials. The framework relates selected structures of particle physics and gravity within a common geometric setting; their further dynamical and phenomenological development remains open.

</details>

Abstract: We give a fourth-order Calabi-Yau differential operator, which seems to have been missed by earlier tabulations. Conclusion: Pb替代能够在NiBi₃中稳定一种异常增强的低维（表面）超导态，为研究体相超导体边界上的超导电性提供了平台。

Abstract: Stabilizing two-dimensional superconductivity in a bulk superconducting material provides a unique route to exploring the interplay between superconducting condensates of different dimensionalities. Here, we present evidence for a distinct surface-associated superconducting state in Pb-substituted NiBi$_3$. NiBi$_{2.8}$Pb$_{0.2}$ exhibits a resistive onset at $T_{c,\mathrm{onset}}^{*}\approx8.1$~K, nearly twice the bulk superconducting transition temperature of approximately 4.1~K. The absence of a corresponding Meissner response or specific-heat anomaly at the higher-temperature transition supports its non-bulk origin. A cusp-like angular dependence of the critical field, well described by the two-dimensional Tinkham model, together with nonlinear current--voltage characteristics and signatures of a Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, establishes the low-dimensional character of this superconducting state. Remarkably, the surface-associated superconductivity persists well beyond the bulk upper critical field, reaching a maximum ratio of $H_{c3}/H_{c2}\approx4.3$, far exceeding the conventional Saint-James--de Gennes value of 1.695. These results demonstrate that Pb substitution can stabilize an anomalously enhanced low-dimensional superconducting state in NiBi$_3$, providing a platform for investigating superconductivity at the boundary of a bulk superconductor.

</details>

Only for $T_{c,\min}=0$ does it become a power law, $Δλ\propto T^n$ with $n\approx1.7$, but then the onset of screening drops to $T_p=T_{c,\max}/2$. Weighting the $T_{c}$ distribution towards $T_c\to0$ (more low-$T_{c}$ domains) can bring $n$ closer to 1 ($n\approx1.2$), but simultaneously pushes the apparent transition, $T_p$, more than an order of magnitude below $T_{c,\max}$. For d-wave domains, the linear term survives, and its relative slope is enhanced almost fourfold for $T_{c,\min}=0$.

Abstract: Motivated by the recent measurement of high-temperature superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate system La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ in the bulk as well as thin film samples, we seek to understand the role of the interlayer coupling in this system in dictating the pairing symmetry. Many proposals posit the existence of an $s_\pm$ superconducting phase in close competition with the mroe conventional cuprate-like $d$-wave state in both weak- and strong-coupling theories. However, at least in the latter, the effects of Coulomb repulsion and the double occupancy constraint have been largely neglected. In this manuscript, we propose a mean-field fractionalization treatment that captures the full $SU(2)$ particle-hole symmetry of the t-J model at half-filling, and show how a systematic treatment of this new symmetry leads to an added Coulomb pseudopotential constraint - one that is absent in the usual $U(1)$ treatment. We then apply this new fractionalization scheme to a simplified bilayer t-J model of La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$, showing how the Coulomb pseudopotential suppresses the existence of $s_\pm$ symmetry in favor of $d$-wave pairing.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在动力学平均场理论（DMFT）框架下，构建并计算含自旋轨道耦合的真实三轨道t2g Hubbard模型，得到相互作用-温度相图；进而分析能隙跨Néel温度的行为、谱函数的自旋极化子特征以及光学电导率结构。

Result: 两种材料均处于巡游电子与局域电子协同稳定反铁磁序的中间机制，其中Ba2IrO4比Sr2IrO4关联性略强；能隙行为和自旋极化子特征均不能唯一甄别该协作机制，但光学电导率的双峰结构仅在该机制下出现，可作可靠指纹。

Conclusion: 通过将两种化合物纳入统一相图，本文调和了看似矛盾的实验与理论结果，为214铱酸盐及其他自旋轨道耦合关联材料的研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The $5d^5$ Ruddlesden-Popper iridates Sr$_2$IrO$_4$ and Ba$_2$IrO$_4$ have attracted considerable attention as spin-orbit entangled analogues of the cuprates, yet the origin of their antiferromagnetic insulating state, of Slater or of Mott-Heisenberg type, remains debated. We find that both materials sit in an intermediate regime in which itinerant and localized electrons cooperate in stabilizing the antiferromagnetic order, with Ba$_2$IrO$_4$ slightly more correlated than Sr$_2$IrO$_4$. We obtain this by computing, within dynamical mean-field theory, the interaction-temperature phase diagram of a realistic three-orbital $t_{2g}$ Hubbard model with spin-orbit coupling. Our study shows that neither the behavior of the gap across the Néel temperature nor the spin-polaron features of the spectral function unambiguously identify the cooperative regime, whereas the two-peak structure of the optical conductivity is reproduced only there and therefore constitutes a reliable fingerprint. By placing both compounds in a common phase diagram, our results reconcile seemingly contradictory experimental and theoretical reports, and provide a unified framework for future studies of the 214 iridates and of other spin-orbit coupled correlated materials.

TL;DR: 本文在无穷大 U 的锯齿（sawtooth）Hubbard 链中确立了一种纯粹由动力学阻挫驱动的磁约束机制：掺杂一个空穴时，基态由 M 个极化三重态二聚体包围一段长度为 L−M 的连通单重态区域组成，外磁场选取 M 从而决定约束长度。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否能在不需要无序或交换产生的弦张力的情况下实现磁约束，即寻找一种仅由动能阻挫（量子干涉）导致的约束机制。

Method: 在有限开放边界、U=∞ 的锯齿 Hubbard 链（L 个原胞）中掺杂一个空穴，利用解析/精确方法研究其基态结构：构造由极化三重态二聚体与连通单重态区域组成的基态，并分析空穴在单重态区域中的驻波行为及其向三重态背景的指数衰减尾巴。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了磁约束的一个全新机制——纯动力学阻挫（破坏性量子干涉）即可产生有效的反射势垒，无需无序或弦张力；并通过精确可解模型揭示了约束长度的场依赖性、紧致局域态的共振条件以及零场与有限场下热力学极限行为的差异。

Abstract: We establish an exact mechanism of magnetic confinement driven purely by kinetic frustration. In a finite open sawtooth $U=\infty$ Hubbard chain of $L$ unit cells doped with one hole, the ground state at magnetization $M$ contains $M$ polarized triplet dimers surrounding a connected singlet region of length $\ell_{\rm s}=L-M$. A magnetic field selects $M$ and hence the confinement length. The hole forms a standing wave in the singlet region with exponential tails in the triplet background. Confinement requires neither disorder nor an exchange-generated string tension: destructive interference between direct and apex-mediated hopping makes the triplet bonds reflecting. At $t_2/t_1=[2+4\cos(π/(\ell_{\rm s}+2))]^{-1/2}$, they become perfectly reflecting, producing a position-degenerate compact localized state. At every fixed field $0<h<h_{\rm sat}$, the singlet region remains finite as $L\to\infty$ and shrinks stepwise with increasing field, whereas at $h=0$ it spans the system.

</details>

Abstract: The spin-1/2 kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet (AFM) provides a paradigmatic platform for realizing quantum spin-liquid (QSL) and magnetization-plateau states. Experimental progress, however, has been hindered by structural disorder, whose effects on these quantum states remain elusive. A key experimental challenge is therefore to connect intrinsic and disorder-dominated regimes through controlled disorder and identify emergent degrees of freedom associated with disorder. Here we introduce controlled point defects through electron irradiation into the spin-1/2 kagome AFM YCu3(OH)6.5Br2.5 (YCOB) and track the resulting evolution of its low-energy magnetic states. High-field magnetic torque measurements reveal that the 1/9 and 1/3 magnetization plateaus are strongly suppressed by only ~0.05% irradiation-induced defects, demonstrating that these delicate quantum many-body states are extraordinarily fragile against dilute disorder. Moreover, the irradiation induces a sign reversal of the magnetic torque only at low temperatures and low fields, indicating the emergence of correlated defect-induced moments rather than isolated free spins. Remarkably, the characteristic scaling of the magnetic specific heat, absent in pristine YCOB, progressively emerges with increasing disorder. These findings establish dilute disorder as an effective tuning parameter that drives the kagome AFM from intrinsic quantum states toward a disorder-dominated regime. The contrasting responses further identify pristine YCOB as an exceptionally clean kagome AFM for accessing intrinsic quantum states.

Abstract: Controlling charge--spin conversion within a single magnetic material is important for compact spintronic devices. Using first-principles calculations, symmetry analysis, and Wannier-based linear response, we study the Edelstein and spin Hall responses of sliding-ferroelectric bilayer \ce{Fe3GaTe2}. Two degenerate ground states with opposite interlayer sliding vectors and out-of-plane dipole moments are separated by a calculated switching barrier of about $16.3$~meV per unit cell. Magnetization orientation determines the allowed current-induced spin components, while ferroelectric switching induces component-selective sign reversals governed by $M_z\mathcal T$ for in-plane magnetization and by $M_z$ for out-of-plane magnetization. The Edelstein response is strongly energy dependent, with spin accumulation approaching $2~\hbar$ for $\mathbf m\parallel+y$ under a reference field of $1$~V/Å. Momentum-resolved analysis links the TR-even and TR-odd Edelstein responses to $Γ$-centered constant-energy contours and localized interband features, respectively. Spin-conductivity contributions show the opposite momentum-space character, with broad sign-competing spin Berry curvature for TR-even and narrow Fermi-surface contours for TR-odd. The transverse spin Hall conductivity reaches $\sim10^{3}\,(\hbar/e)(\mathrm{S/cm})$ and depends strongly on ferroelectric state and magnetization direction. Magnetization rotations in the $xy$ and $xz$ planes reveal symmetry-allowed higher angular harmonics in both spin accumulation and spin-current responses. These results establish sliding-ferroelectric bilayer \ce{Fe3GaTe2} as a tunable platform for reconfigurable spin accumulation and spin-current generation.

Abstract: Flat-band systems provide a promising platform for exploring exotic correlated phases owing to quenched kinetic energy and enhanced interaction effects. In this work, we investigate the flat-band superfluid transition in the attractive kagome Hubbard model using large-scale determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations accelerated by fast Fourier transforms and delay updates. Through systematic calculations of the transition temperature, spin susceptibility, and density compressibility, we establish the finite-temperature phase diagram at the flat-band filling $ρ=5/3$ and find a clear linear scaling of the superfluid transition temperature with the interaction strength, $T_{\mathrm{c}}=0.0314|U|$. Our results reveal a wide separation of temperature scales between local pair formation and global phase coherence, which stabilizes a broad intermediate supercompressible bosonic fluid region of preformed pairs that bridges the normal Fermi gas and the flat-band superfluid. We further demonstrate that both the transition temperature and density compressibility are significantly enhanced in the flat band compared to van Hove fillings. Our findings highlight the essential role of flat-band-enhanced pair-density fluctuations in separating the pairing and phase-coherence scales, offering clear experimental signatures for probing flat-band superfluidity and superconductivity in cold-atom and kagome-material platforms.

Abstract: We investigate the $1/9$ magnetization plateau of the spin-$1/2$ kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet using infinite projected entangled-pair states and identify a spin-density-wave state with a $27$-site unit cell and a lower variational energy than previous approaches. In contrast to higher-magnetization plateaus, which are well described by localized magnons on nonoverlapping hexagons and preserve a sixfold rotational symmetry, the $1/9$ plateau only keeps a threefold rotational symmmetry and consists of a tiling by three types of $9$-site clusters related by $\pm2π/3$ rotations. Unlike conventional electrical magnetochiral anisotropy, which is driven by an external magnetic field, our spontaneous magnetic diode effect arises from an internal noncoplanar spin texture with vanishing net magnetization. Symmetry analysis and model calculations show that the effect originates from the interplay of noncoplanar spin order and spin-orbit coupling, which generates a chiral, time-reversal-symmetry-breaking electronic response. Abstract: Finite-size effects are ubiquitous in quantum matter, yet typically invisible in the thermodynamic limit; when system size is restricted, boundaries and confinement reveal modified spectra, correlations, and emergent phenomena. *Naomichi Sato,Makoto Naka,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用多轨道Hubbard模型结合Peierls型电子-晶格耦合，研究了固态氧中结构序与磁序的相互作用，发现反铁磁序与三种键序共存相具有丰富的对称性破缺特性，可产生磁电效应、交变磁自旋劈裂和压磁响应等实验可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态氧是研究结构与磁自由度相互作用的独特平台：每个O₂分子具有S=1自旋，弱的范德华分子间作用带来显著结构柔性，且小分子间跃迁积分使其成为多轨道强关联电子体系。近期高压实验提示固态氧可能存在磁序与结构序共存，需要理论模型来揭示其电子态及磁、介电响应，为实验提供可观测的判别信号。

Method: 采用多轨道Hubbard模型，并引入Peierls型电子-晶格耦合，以最小化描述固态氧中结构与磁自由度的相互作用。通过Hartree-Fock近似求解基态，获得相图，并分析各相的对称性（时间反演、空间反演），进一步计算磁电响应、自旋劈裂与压磁响应等性质。

Result: 当在位库仑相互作用强时出现反铁磁序；作用弱时出现三种非磁键序态；在中等相互作用强度下，反铁磁序与三种键序分别共存。三种共存相均破缺时间反演对称性，其中一个保持空间反演对称，另两个同时破缺空间反演对称。两个破缺反演对称的相表现出线性磁电响应；保持反演对称的相则表现出交变磁（altermagnetic）自旋劈裂和压磁响应。

Conclusion: 这些磁电、交变磁自旋劈裂及压磁响应可作为高压固态氧中磁序与结构序共存的实验特征，为近期实验所提示的共存现象提供理论鉴别依据。

Abstract: Solid oxygen provides a unique platform for studying the interplay between structural and magnetic degrees of freedom, as each O$_2$ molecule possesses an $S=1$ spin and weak intermolecular van der Waals interactions allow substantial structural flexibility. In addition, because of its small intermolecular hopping integrals, this system is known to be a strongly correlated electron system with a multiorbital nature. In this study, we examine electronic states and magnetic and dielectric responses using a multiorbital Hubbard model with Peierls-type electron-lattice coupling. This model provides a minimal description of the interplay between structural and magnetic degrees of freedom in solid oxygen. We apply the Hartree-Fock approximation to obtain the ground-state phase diagram. Antiferromagnetic order appears when the onsite Coulomb interaction is strong, whereas three nonmagnetic bond-ordered states emerge when it is weak. At intermediate interaction strengths, antiferromagnetic order coexists with each of the three bond orders. These three coexistence phases all break time-reversal symmetry; one preserves spatial inversion symmetry, whereas the other two also break it. The two inversion-breaking phases exhibit linear magnetoelectric responses, whereas the inversion-symmetric phase exhibits altermagnetic spin splitting and a piezomagnetic response. These responses may provide experimental signatures of coexisting magnetic and structural orders in high-pressure solid oxygen suggested by recent experiments.

</details>


### [100] [Orbital-selective correlations in kagome flat bands of Co$_3$Sn$_2$S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35255)
*Rongxiang Zhu,Huaiqiang Wang,Haijun Zhang,Jianzhou Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示 Co3Sn2S2 铁磁外尔半金属中近费米能级平带的起源：并非重费米子式的动力学重整化，而是轨道/自旋依赖的自能将 d_{x^2-y^2} 主导的几何 kagome 平带推向费米面，进而增强局域贝里曲率，产生能量依赖的反常霍尔电导与巨大反常能斯特效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验光谱在 Co3Sn2S2 费米能级附近观测到平带特征，但传统 DFT 计算中并不存在该平带，需要解释这一平带的物理起源及其对拓扑输运的影响。

Method: 采用 DFT+动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）结合最小 kagome 模型，分析轨道与自旋依赖的自能效应，并计算局域贝里曲率、反常霍尔电导及反常能斯特效应。

Result: 发现费米能级附近的平带源于 d_{x^2-y^2} 主导的几何 kagome 平带因轨道/自旋依赖自能而被推向费米能级，而非重费米子式动力学带重整化；该平带增强局域贝里曲率，导致强烈能量依赖的反常霍尔电导率和巨大反常能斯特效应。

Conclusion: 建立了一种轨道选择性关联机制，可将几何平带定位于费米能级，并提出反常热电输运可作为探测平带诱导拓扑的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Geometrical flat bands provide an ideal platform for studying electronic correlations and quantum-geometric effects. Although the ferromagnetic Weyl semimetal \CoSnS is generally considered a moderately correlated system, recent spectroscopy experiments have revealed a flat-band feature near the Fermi level ($\EF$) that is absent in conventional density functional theory (DFT) calculations. Using DFT plus dynamical mean-field theory and a minimal kagome model, we show that this feature arises not from heavy-fermion-like dynamical band renormalization, but from the shifting of a $d_{x^2-y^2}$ dominated geometrical kagome flat band toward $\EF$ driven by orbital- and spin-dependent self-energies. The relocated flat band enhances the local Berry curvature near $\EF$, resulting in a strongly energy-dependent anomalous Hall conductivity and a giant anomalous Nernst effect. Our findings establish an orbital-selective correlation mechanism for positioning geometrical flat bands at $\EF$, and propose anomalous thermoelectric transport as a sensitive probe of flat band induced topology.

</details>


### [101] [Anisotropic magneto-elastic coupling in the quasi-2D Ising magnet Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$ and thermodynamic signature of low-energy orbital excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35341)
*Waldemar Hergett,Kevin Ackermann,Sven Spachmann,Martin Jonak,Mahmoud Abdel-Hafiez,Hend Shahed,Michael Merz,Maurits W. Haverkort,Rüdiger Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过热膨胀与比热测量并结合数值研究，揭示了准二维伊辛型磁体Li₂FeSiO₄中自旋、晶格与轨道自由度的关联，分离出轨道能隙及其压力依赖，并分析了磁序及短程关联的磁弹耦合与各向异性应变响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究准二维伊辛型磁体Li₂FeSiO₄中自旋、晶格与轨道自由度的耦合作用，特别是轨道激发对热力学性质的贡献、磁弹耦合以及短程磁序的起源。

Method: 结合热膨胀与比热测量，开展热力学分析和Grüneisen分析，并辅以第一性原理（DFT）数值计算；从实验数据中分离轨道信号、量化能隙及其压力依赖，并测定长程磁序相变处热膨胀反常所对应的各向异性单轴应变依赖。

Result: 轨道能隙Δ约300–350 K，压力依赖∂lnΔ/∂p_c≈−4.6%/GPa，与DFT预测高度吻合；长程磁序伴随显著磁弹耦合，T_N的各向异性单轴应变响应以面内c轴响应为主(∂lnT_N/∂p_c≈15%/GPa)，a轴(负)与面外b轴(正)的压力依赖小得多；短程磁序远高于T_N存在，但由不同于T_N的能标驱动，表现为非单调面内畸变、Grüneisen参数符号翻转及Grüneisen标度失效。

Conclusion: Li₂FeSiO₄中轨道、自旋与晶格自由度强烈耦合，轨道能隙及其压力依赖可由实验精确确定并与理论一致；长程磁序的磁弹耦合具有显著各向异性，而短程磁序由独立的能标驱动，不能简单以Grüneisen标度统一描述。

Abstract: The relevance of spin, lattice and orbital degrees of freedom in the quasi-2D Ising-like magnet Li$_2$FeSiO$_4$ is demonstrated by thermal expansion and specific heat measurements supported by numerical studies. By means of thermodynamic and Grüneisen analysis, we separate the orbital signatures and quantify the energy gap and its pressure dependence from the experimental data as $Δ\sim 300 - 350$ K and $\partial \ln Δ/\partial p_{\rm c} \approx -4.6$ %/GPa). Both values excellently agree with the theoretical DFT predictions. The thermal expansion anomalies associated with the onset of long-range magnetic order imply significant magneto-elastic coupling and are used to determine the anisotropic uniaxial strain dependencies of $T_{\rm N}$. They are dominated by the in-plane (i.e., $c$ axis) response $(\partial \ln{ T_{\rm N}} )/\partial p_{\rm c} \simeq 15$ %/GPa while the (negative) $a$ axis and (positive) out-of-plane ($b$ axis) pressure dependencies are much smaller. Short-range magnetic order extends well above $T_{\rm N}$ but is not driven by the same energy scale as $T_{\rm N}$ as evidenced by the observation of non-monotonic in-plane distortion, sign-change of the Grüneisen parameter, and failure of Grüneisen scaling.

</details>


### [102] [Collinear and Coplanar Altermagnetism in a Penrose Quasicrystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35855)
*Bai-He Yao,Zhi-Yan Shao,Ye Cao,Fan Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将此前基于群论对准晶共线磁性的分类推广到一般磁态，以彭罗斯晶格为例指出：属于D5点群二维不可约表示（2D-IRRP）的所有磁态（含共线与共面态）均对应交变磁性（AM）。其零磁矩由群论正交性定理（不同IRRP基函数相互正交）保护，而非传统共线AM中的旋转/镜面反射对称。通过彭罗斯-哈伯德模型的线性响应计算与金兹堡-朗道分析，研究了这些态的占据参数空间及极化方向（平行或垂直，分别对应共线/共面态）。群论正交补偿机制与PT对称破缺最终导致AM，可由自旋分辨ARPES和自旋电导验证，且可推广到其他准晶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将准晶中共线磁性的群论分类推广到一般磁态，揭示准晶中不同于传统晶体AM的、由群论正交性而非点群对称操作保护的新型交变磁性机制，并预测其在参数空间中的普遍性。

Method: 以彭罗斯晶格为例，基于D5点群的群论分类确定所有属于二维不可约表示（2D-IRRP）的磁态；采用单轨道彭罗斯-哈伯德模型进行基于线性响应理论的计算，确定不同磁态占据的参数空间；进一步结合金兹堡-朗道理论分析二维IRRP中两个基磁态的极化方向（平行或垂直），并讨论自旋分辨ARPES与自旋电导作为实验验证手段。

Result: 所有属于D5点群二维不可约表示（2D-IRRP）的磁态（含共线和共面态）均为交变磁态（AM）；其零磁矩由群论正交性定理（不同IRRP基函数正交）保护，而非传统AM的旋转或镜面反射。二维IRRP态占据彭罗斯-哈伯德模型参数空间的最大部分；两个基磁态的极化方向可平行或垂直，分别对应共线或共面磁态。群论正交补偿机制结合PT对称破缺导致AM，并可通过自旋分辨ARPES的自旋劈裂和自旋电导验证。

Conclusion: 在准晶（如彭罗斯晶格）中，属于D5点群二维不可约表示的磁态普遍呈现交变磁性，其零磁矩保护机制为群论正交性定理而非传统的对称操作；这类共线和共面AM可推广至其他准晶，并可通过自旋分辨ARPES与自旋电导实验证实。

Abstract: We generalize the previous group-theory based classification of collinear magnetism in the quasicrystal (QC) to general magnetic states. Taking the Penrose lattice as an example, we find that all magnetic states belonging to 2D irreducible representations (IRRPs) of the $D_5$ point group, including collinear states and coplanar ones, correspond to altermagnetism (AM). Unlike conventional collinear AM wherein zero magnetism is protected by, e.g. rotation or mirror reflection, which globally flip the spin in the magnetic pattern, the zero magnetism in these 2D-IRRP states is instead protected by a special group-theory-orthogonality theorem that basis functions belonging to different IRRPs are always mutually orthogonal. Taking the single-orbital Penrose-Hubbard as an example, our linear-response theory based calculations show that the largest portion of the parameter space is occupied by these 2D-IRRP states, and our further Ginzburg-Landau theory based analysis reveals that the polarization directions of the two basis magnetic states in the 2D IRRPs can be either parallel or perpendicular, leading to collinear or coplanar magnetic states. The special group-theory-orthogonality compensation mechanism and the absence of the PT symmetry finally leads to AM states, which are to be verified by the spin split in the spin-dependent angle-resolved-photoemission-spectrum and the spin conductance. These intriguing collinear and coplanar AM can also emerge in other QCs.

</details>

### [103] [Controlling transport with structured long-range hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33393)
*Boris Gurevich,Michael H. Kolodrubetz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究一维无序链中最近邻跳跃叠加长程跳跃项的输运行为，发现均匀长程跳跃幅度会在弱无序区强烈抑制退局域化，而随机化长程跳跃幅度可消除该抑制，从而增强输运并改变系统对无序的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序与长程跳跃的联合效应对于确定低维量子系统的局域化与输运至关重要，但其相互作用尚未被充分理解，尤其是弱无序区出现的"无序增强输运"现象的微观起源。

Method: 在含对角无序的一维链上，引入最近邻跳跃并叠加长程跳跃项，其中长程耦合采用位点相关的、均匀或随机的振幅；通过在Anderson本征态基下的解析求解，揭示输运效率变化的微观机制。

Result: 发现均匀长程跳跃幅度结合最近邻跳跃会在较大范围的弱无序区强烈抑制退局域化；此前建立的"无序增强输运"现象可理解为系统从该强抑制状态中恢复退局域化的过程；在长程跳跃幅度中引入随机性可消除弱无序区的强抑制，成为一种额外的输运增强机制，并定性改变系统对无序的响应。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃幅度的统计性质（均匀或随机）对一维无序系统的输运有决定性影响：均匀幅度造成弱无序强抑制退局域化，随机化幅度则可消除该抑制并进一步增强输运，为低维量子输运的调控提供了新机制。

Abstract: The combined effects of disorder and long-range hopping play an important role in determining localization and transport in low-dimensional quantum systems. We study transport in a one-dimensional disordered chain with nearest-neighbor hopping supplemented by a long-range hopping term. The long-range coupling is characterized by site-dependent amplitudes that may be either uniform or random. We investigate the interplay between diagonal disorder and long-range hopping, characterizing the resulting transport efficiency. We find that nearest-neighbor hopping in the presence of uniform long-range hopping amplitudes strongly suppresses delocalization over a range of weak disorder. The disorder-enhanced transport regime, established in previous studies, can be understood here as the recovery of delocalization from this strongly suppressed regime. An analytical solution in the Anderson eigenstate basis reveals the microscopic origin of this behavior. In particular, we show that the low-disorder regime of strongly suppressed delocalization can be eliminated by introducing randomness into the long-range hopping amplitudes, providing an additional mechanism for enhancing transport and qualitatively changing the response of the system to disorder.

</details>


### [104] [Dynamic Pseudogap Model: Analytic Results vs Monte Carlo Numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33436)
*Michael V. Sadovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文对比了动态赝能隙模型的解析结果与蒙特卡洛精确数值解，验证了在相干与准静态两种极限下解析方法的适用性及限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态赝能隙模型此前已给出电子谱密度的解析结果，但这些结果建立在若干近似（如对易近似、忽略 Magnus 型修正）之上，其精度与适用范围尚不明确，需要通过精确数值解加以检验。

Method: 对散射电子格林函数分别采用双泊松级数展开（含历史依赖的 Bogoliubov 能隙）和精确静态连分式解两种近似，并与蒙特卡洛方法得到的精确数值谱密度进行对比；考察了公度（实）与非公度（复）涨落，以及模型的广泛参数范围。

Result: 在相干区，双泊松级数能描述动态边带形成；随相干性丧失，理论过渡到由连分式解描述的准静态涨落能隙区；蒙特卡洛解克服了解析近似的限制（如对易近似中忽略的 Magnus 型修正），确定了各近似的适用边界，并确认连分式解在静态极限下的精确性。

Conclusion: 解析表达式在其适用范围内与蒙特卡洛数值结果符合良好，证实了动态赝能隙形成的整体定性物理图像；未来可借助精确数值解进一步拓展解析方法的有效区域。

Abstract: Recently proposed dynamic pseudogap model analytical results for spectral density of an electron scattered by random time-dependent Gaussian field representing fluctuations with finite nesting vector ${\bf Q}$, characteristic oscillation frequency $ω_0$ and damping $γ$, leading to pseudogap formation, are compared with exact numerical solution by Monte Carlo (MC) approach. We consider both commensurate (real) and incommensurate (complex) fluctuations, for a wide range of parameters of the model. Electron Green's function is approximated either by a double Poisson series with history-dependent Bogoliubov gaps or by exact static continued fraction solution. In the coherent regime double-series representation provides description of dynamic sideband formation in the spectral density. As coherence is progressively lost, the theory crosses over to the quasistatic fluctuating-gap regime described by the exact continued-fraction solution. The exact MC solution overcomes the limitations of analytic approach, such as neglect of Magnus-type corrections in commuting approximation, and establish the limits of analytic approach, as well as confirming the exactness of continued fraction solution in static limit. In general, analytic expressions demonstrate rather satisfactory agreement with exact MC numerics within their limits, confirming the overall qualitative physical picture of dynamic pseudogap formation.

</details>


### [105] [Matter that learns what matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34428)
*Natalia G. Berloff*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种模型光网络中材料能“学会”该关注什么：每个链路根据行动结果形成持久记录，记录偏置信息来源是否被咨询及其对存储答案的影响强度；但共振读出使学习脆弱，作者推导出边界并设计学习规则，通过局部状态在行动失败时强化负向写入来释放过时记录，从而完成学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究材料/网络能否不预先指定信息源，而是依据行动结果自主决定“关注什么”，即让经验塑造未来可利用的信息，并解决共振读出导致的脆弱性及过时记录阻碍更新的问题。

Method: 构建模型光网络：每个链路中的持久记录由行动结果训练，记录偏置某来源是否被咨询及改变存储答案的强度；采用共振读出；推导相关信号生效、无关信号被拒、存储答案保持可修正的边界；依据边界设计限制强化的学习规则；引入在行动不成功后强化负向写入的局部状态以释放过时记录。

Result: 推导出使相关信号引导答案、无关信号被拒、存储答案可修正的边界；依据边界的学习规则在模拟中能获得有用记录并保持其功能；当相关性改变时，过时记录会阻碍自身被替换，而引入的局部状态（失败后强化负向写入）能释放过时记录，更常地完成学习序列。

Conclusion: 记忆的读出方式应决定其写入方式；共振读出下的强化可能使记录越过共振而失效，因此需要限定强化并结合失败时负向写入的局部状态，以维持可修正性并成功适应变化的相关性。

Abstract: Can a material learn what deserves its attention? We show that it can in a model optical network, in which action outcomes train a persistent record in each link. The record biases whether a source is consulted and sets how strongly it changes a stored answer, so useful sources gain attention and influence without being identified in advance. The network's experience thus shapes the information available for its future learning. Resonant readouts make this learning fragile: reinforcement can carry a record past resonance, where it persists but its influence weakens. We derive bounds within which relevant signals guide the answer, irrelevant signals are rejected and stored answers remain open to revision. These bounds guide a learning rule that limits reinforcement as records form; in simulations, it acquires useful records and keeps them functional. When relevance changes, an obsolete record can block its own replacement. A local state that strengthens negative writing after unsuccessful actions releases obsolete records and completes the learning sequence more often. How a memory is read should decide how it is written.

</details>


### [106] [Symmetry-driven in-situ physical adjoint computing beyond reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34637)
*Cunshen Li,Rong Li,Jiongning Che,Cheng-Zhen Wang,Liang Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出对称性驱动的原位物理伴随计算（Sym-iPAC），在非互易系统中通过三类转置对称性（局部几何置换、复规范重加权、Schur约化揭示的等效端口动力学中的潜在对称性）获取伴随响应，从而突破传统伴随优化对互易性的依赖，并在可编程非互易微波网络上实现闭环优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性通过定向传输丰富了波控制与信息处理，但传统的原位物理伴随优化依赖互易性来获取伴随响应，难以直接应用于非互易系统；因此需要一种不依赖互易性的新途径来获取伴随梯度。

Method: 提出对称性驱动的原位物理伴随计算（Sym-iPAC），利用三类转置对称性在原硬件上获取伴随响应：① 局部几何置换（local geometric permutations）；② 复规范重加权（complex gauge reweighting）；③ 通过 Schur 约简揭示的等效端口动力学中的潜在对称性（latent symmetry）。并在可编程非互易微波网络上通过实验验证这些转置关系并计算伴随梯度，实现闭环优化。

Result: 实验在可编程非互易微波网络上验证了各类转置关系有效成立，并能据此获取伴随梯度，进而实现系统的闭环优化，证明 Sym-iPAC 可在非互易系统中运行。

Conclusion: 实验可及的转置对称性为超越互易性的原位物理伴随计算提供了可行路径，将物理伴随优化推广至非互易系统。

Abstract: Nonreciprocity enriches wave control and information processing through directional transport, yet challenges conventional in-situ physical adjoint optimization, which relies on reciprocity to acquire adjoint responses. Here we demonstrate symmetry-driven in-situ physical adjoint computing (Sym-iPAC) in nonreciprocal systems, which enables access to adjoint responses on the original hardware through three classes of transpose symmetry: local geometric permutations, complex gauge reweighting, and latent symmetry in effective port dynamics revealed by Schur reduction. Experiments with programmable nonreciprocal microwave networks validate these transpose relations and yield adjoint gradients, enabling closed-loop optimization. These results establish experimentally accessible transpose symmetry as a route to in-situ physical adjoint computing beyond reciprocity.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

### [107] [Chemistry-resolved disagreement between Materials Project-compatible PBE(+U) and r2SCAN stability verdicts in the GNoME materials database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33101)
*Karthikeya Aduru*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文对 GNoME 数据集声称的 r2SCAN 稳定性进行了按化学组成分解的再分析，发现总体稳定性比例掩盖了巨大的化学间差异，且 Materials Project 的 Hubbard-U 元素列表与两种工作流之间的不一致强烈相关，组成信息可有效补充凸包距离以优化验证预算分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNoME 的稳定性标签基于 GGA/GGA+U 工作流，其作者报告的 r2SCAN 稳定性比例（二元/三元 84%、四元 86.8%）是聚合数字，可能掩盖不同化学体系间的显著差异；作者希望剖析这一聚合结果，检验是否存在系统性偏差（如 Hubbard-U 处理）以及如何更高效地分配验证预算。

Method: 在 139,541 个附带 r2SCAN 重算结果的 GNoME 材料子集（由数据集作者选定而非随机抽样）上，按化学组成分解聚合稳定性；以 8,429 个结构原型代理簇进行聚类 bootstrap 获得区间估计；使用聚类稳健逻辑回归（含连续 PBE 分解能量的自然三次样条）评估 Hubbard-U 元素与分歧的关联；在留出簇划分及 20% 预算下比较"组成+凸包距离"与"仅凸包距离"两种验证策略的召回率；并与既往目录材料做探索性对比。

Result: 聚合稳定性掩盖了固定元素数目下高达 26.7 个百分点的差异：从纯四元金属间化合物（无镧系、无 3d）的 4.0% [3.3, 4.7] 到含 3d 无镧系四元氧化物的 30.7% [27.7, 33.8]；在"All"参考集读法下二者分别为 14.5% 和 37.4%，仍有 22.9 个百分点差异。Hubbard-U 元素的出现与分歧强相关（聚类稳健优势比 3.45 [3.13, 3.80]），且无论选择性施加 U 还是凸包邻近度都无法解释该关联；加入 PBE 分解能量样条后 117,018 材料模型中优势比仅从 3.53 降至 3.43 [3.07, 3.83]。20% 预算下"组成+凸包距离"召回 45.8% 的分歧，优于仅凸包距离的 40.4%。与既有材料的探索性对比在不同群体间方向相反。

Conclusion: 作者强调其度量的是两个工作流（GGA/GGA+U 与 r2SCAN）之间的分歧而非准确性，聚合数字会掩盖重要的化学依赖差异；Hubbard-U 相关的不一致系统性存在且难以用简单协变量解释；在分配验证预算时，组成信息应作为凸包邻近度的补充而非替代。

Abstract: GNoME's stability labels rest on a Materials Project-compatible GGA/GGA+U workflow. Its authors report that 84% of discovered binaries and ternaries retain a negative decomposition energy under r2SCAN and 86.8% of tested quaternaries remain stable on the r2SCAN hull. We resolve that aggregate by chemistry over the 139,541 GNoME materials released with r2SCAN recomputations, a subset the dataset authors selected rather than sampled, using cluster bootstrap intervals over 8,429 structural-prototype proxy clusters.
  The aggregate conceals a 26.7-point spread at fixed element count, from 4.0% [3.3, 4.7] for plain quaternary intermetallics (no lanthanide, no 3d metal) to 30.7% [27.7, 33.8] for lanthanide-free 3d-containing quaternary oxides, a pair identified post hoc against a prospectively fixed threshold. Under our reading of the release's "All" reference set the two cells sit at 14.5% and 37.4%, preserving a 22.9-point spread. Presence of a Materials Project Hubbard-U-list element is strongly associated with disagreement, conditional on the other covariates, in a model over all 139,541 materials (cluster-robust odds ratio 3.45 [3.13, 3.80]). Neither selective U application nor hull proximity explains the association away: disagreement stays elevated outside the U-treated regime, and a natural cubic spline in the continuous PBE decomposition energy moves the odds ratio in the 117,018-material model only from 3.53 to 3.43 [3.07, 3.83]. For allocating a verification budget, composition supplements hull proximity rather than replacing it: over held-out cluster splits at a 20% budget, the two together surface 45.8% of disagreements against 40.4% for hull distance alone. An exploratory comparison against previously catalogued materials changes direction between populations. We measure disagreement between two workflows, not accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [On the Doppler Effect in Static Materials: Vortices and Black, White and Worm-Hole Like Wave Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33141)
*Tristan Lawrie,Simon Horsley*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种在静态材料中模拟多普勒效应的方法，通过设计长度随传播方向变化的波导网络，等效实现源以速度 v 运动时的波压缩与展宽，并用该方法构建了涡旋、模拟黑洞和虫洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在静态材料中再现运动源产生的多普勒效应，为在无实际运动的系统中模拟波动现象（如涡旋、黑洞、虫洞）提供实验平台。

Method: 构建薄波导网络，使每条长度为 ℓ 的边替换为一对长度分别为 ℓ/(1−v/c) 和 ℓ/(1+v/c) 的波导，分别对应波的两个传播方向，从而模拟源以速度 v 运动时波前压缩与展缩的效果；在晶格上复现运动源的场型。

Result: 成功在静态波导网络中实现等效多普勒效应，并据此设计了涡旋、模拟黑洞和虫洞的场型。

Conclusion: 通过方向依赖的波导长度设计，可在静态材料中精确模拟运动源的多普勒效应，为研究旋转与引力类比现象提供了新方法。

Abstract: We demonstrate the Doppler effect in a static material. The effect is achieved with a network of thin waveguides whose lengths depend on the direction of travel, chosen to simulate the compression and expansion of the wave experienced by a source travelling with velocity $v$.
