By successfully deviating from the Janovec-Kay-Dunn law, reversal polarization of engineered BaTiO3 (Ps~1.2 uC cm^-2, Vc~0.4 V) is sufficient to switch the coherent magnon transport in the (La,Sr)MnO3, indicating the magnetoelectric heterointerface may serve as a promising candidate for atto-joule control of spin-wave propagation (normalized to a 10*10 nm^2 device). Furthermore, taking advantage of amplitude, phase, and frequency to encode spin waves, all 16 compact Boolean logics with ultralow energy consumption have been designed. The artificial magnetoelectric exchange spring can be broadly applicable to various material choices, offering a versatile platform for developing energy-efficient and integrated magnonic devices.

</details>


### [142] [Optimised growth of epitaxial D019 Mn3Ge(0001) thin films via single target sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34736)
*Servet Ozdemir,Ping-Luen Ho,Callum Brennan-Rich,Matthew Rogers,Philippa Shepley,Ahmet Yagmur,Ismet Gelen,Sehwan Song,Zabeada Aslam,Mannan Ali,Shelly Conroy,Sandrine Heutz,Gavin Burnell,B J Hickey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种在Al2O3(0001)衬底上以Ru(0001)为种子层、从Mn5Ge靶材生长出具有D019对称性的超高质量Mn3Ge薄膜的方法，并确认了其非共线反铁磁序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn3X（X=Sn、Ga、Ge）是有拓扑性质的Weyl半金属，其Kagome晶格使其成为手性非共线反铁磁体。其中Mn3Ge因具有更大Berry曲率而显示出大的反常霍尔电导率，具有自旋电子学应用前景；但这些优良性质只有在其具有D019晶体结构时才能获得，因此需要开发一种易于重复的薄膜生长配方。

Method: 采用磁控溅射等方法，以Mn5Ge为靶材，在Al2O3(0001)衬底上先沉积Ru(0001)种子层，再生长Mn3Ge薄膜，并通过结构表征确认获得D019对称性；随后通过磁化测量验证其磁有序特性。

Result: 成功生长出具有D019对称性的超高质量Mn3Ge薄膜。观测到弱的零场磁化强度(0.0086 ± 0.0002) μB/Mn，且在高磁场下不饱和，奈尔温度为(291 ± 2) K，证实了预期的非共线反铁磁序。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可重复生长的、获得D019结构Mn3Ge薄膜的配方，为进一步研究其拓扑与自旋电子学性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: Materials with chemical identity Mn3X, where X is Sn, Ga, or Ge, are Heusler alloys with intriguing properties that are fundamentally and technologically interesting. They have topological properties with a non-trivial band structure that identify them as Weyl semimetals in the bulk. Their magnetic properties, which give rise to the topological band structure through spin-orbit coupling, are determined by their Kagome lattice structure, making them chiral non-collinear antiferromagnets. Among these materials, Mn3Ge is particularly interesting because it has a larger Berry curvature, leading to a large anomalous Hall conductivity and motivating potential spintronic applications. The desirable properties are only obtained when Mn3Ge has the D019 crystal structure, so producing an easily reproducible growth recipe is the main objective of this paper. Here we show that ultra-high-quality thin films of Mn3Ge with D019 symmetry can be grown from a Mn5Ge target on an Al2O3(0001) substrate with a Ru(0001) seed layer. The expected non-collinear antiferromagnetic order was confirmed by the finding of weak zero-field magnetisation (0.0086 +/- 0.0002) mu_B/Mn that does not saturate at high magnetic fields and has a Neel temperature of (291 +/- 2) K.

</details>


### [143] [A van der Waals Floating-Gate Platform for Broadband Optoelectronic Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34738)
*Thi Phuong Anh Bach,Sangeun Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于ReS2/hBN/InSe范德华异质结构的浮栅突触晶体管，实现了非易失存储与宽带光电学习的一体化集成，可模拟多种突触功能、联想学习及可重构布尔逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代神经形态计算需要能同时集成非易失存储与宽带光电学习的突触器件，但传统浮栅存储器的光学功能有限且可扩展性差，难以满足这一需求。

Method: 作者构建了ReS2/hBN/InSe范德华浮栅突触晶体管：以InSe作为浮栅层实现稳定电荷存储，hBN作为隧穿介电层，ReS2作为光响应沟道。在电学和光学刺激下，通过层间协同工作实现载流子隧穿，从而在同一器件中实现存储与光电学习功能。

Result: 器件实现了大存储窗口、超过10^6的开关电流比、稳定的多级电导态，以及超过10^4秒的可靠保持特性。在可见光至近红外宽带光激发下，实现了可控的突触权重调节，并模拟了短时/长时塑性、双脉冲易化、脉冲幅度与脉宽依赖塑性等基本突触功能；通过光学增强与电学抑制实现了双向权重调制。此外，器件还演示了基于巴甫洛夫范式的高阶联想与辨别条件学习，以及由电-光输入驱动的可重构布尔逻辑。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了多功能范德华浮栅平台，在单个晶体管内集成了非易失存储、宽带光电学习与自适应信息处理，为紧凑、低能耗的神经形态硬件提供了一种有前景的策略。

Abstract: Artificial synapses that integrate nonvolatile memory with broadband optoelectronic learning are essential for next-generation neuromorphic computing. However, their realization remains challenging because conventional floating-gate memories exhibit limited optical functionality and poor scalability. Here, we report a van der Waals ReS2/hBN/InSe floating-gate synaptic transistor that combines efficient charge storage with broadband optoelectronic learning in a single device architecture. The heterostructure incorporates InSe as a floating-gate layer for stable charge storage, hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) as a tunneling dielectric, and ReS2 as a photoresponsive channel, enabling carrier tunneling under electrical and optical stimulation. Through the combined operation of these layers, the device achieves a large memory window, an on/off current ratio exceeding 10^6, stable multilevel conductance states, and reliable retention over 10^4 s. Broadband visible-to-near-infrared optical excitation enables controllable synaptic weight modulation and the emulation of essential synaptic functions, including short- and long-term plasticity, paired-pulse facilitation, and pulse-amplitude- and pulse-duration-dependent plasticity. Optical potentiation and electrical depression further enable bidirectional modulation of synaptic weights. Beyond these synaptic functions, the device demonstrates higher-order learning through associative and discrimination conditioning inspired by the Pavlovian learning paradigm, as well as reconfigurable Boolean logic driven by electro-optical inputs. These results establish a multifunctional van der Waals floating-gate platform that integrates nonvolatile memory, broadband optoelectronic learning, and adaptive information processing within a single transistor, providing a promising strategy for compact, energy-efficient neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [144] [Nonlocal Einstein Relation in Confined Phonon Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35109)
*Guanda Quan,Ronggui Yang,Xin Qian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文推导了一个非局域爱因斯坦关系，将受限通道中的声子流体动力学热导率与集体漂移迁移率跨整个横截面联系起来，揭示非局域性导致本征边界滑移、不同的尺寸标度行为，并通过石墨第一性原理计算验证了其能更准确预测热导率的超线性尺寸依赖及流体动力学峰的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声子流体动力学中，爱因斯坦关系是局域的——漂移诱导的热导率正比于局域漂移迁移率。作者指出现有局域理论在空间受限条件下失效，无法准确描述有限克努森数下迁移率与热导率的关系，也无法解释本征边界滑移、尺寸标度差异及流体动力学峰等现象，因此需要建立超越局域流体力学的微观联系。

Method: 作者推导出非局域爱因斯坦关系 κ_H(y) = ∫ P(y−y′) μ(y′) dy′，其中流体动力学热导率 κ_H(y) 依赖整个通道横截面的集体漂移迁移率 μ(y′)；在小克努森数极限下该理论回退到黏性热方程与局域爱因斯坦关系；随后对石墨进行第一性原理计算，并在不同温度下进行模拟以检验理论预测。

Result: 非局域性产生本征边界滑移，并使漂移迁移率 μ 和流体动力学热导率 κ_H(y) 呈现不同的尺寸标度行为；石墨计算显示非局域性拓宽了热导率呈现超线性尺寸依赖的通道尺寸窗口；温度依赖模拟表明，非局域理论能准确捕捉以弹道电导归一化的热导率中流体动力学峰的涌现及其幅度，而局域理论无法准确预测其大小。

Conclusion: 本文在局域流体力学之外建立了集体声子迁移率与热传导之间的微观联系，证明受限声子输运需要非局域爱因斯坦关系来描述，为理解和预测纳米尺度声子流体动力学行为提供了更准确的理论框架。

Abstract: Einstein relations connect carrier mobility to macroscopic transport coefficients and constitute a fundamental link between microscopic dynamics and transport. In phonon hydrodynamics, this connection is conventionally local: the drift-induced thermal conductivity is proportional to the local drift mobility. Here we show that this relation becomes intrinsically nonlocal under spatial confinement. We derive a nonlocal Einstein relation, $κ_H(y) = \int P(y-y')μ(y'),dy'$, in which hydrodynamic thermal conductivity $κ_H(y)$ at one position depends on collective drift mobility $μ(y')$ across the entire cross-section of the channel. In the small-Knudsen-number limit, our nonlocal theory recovers the viscous heat equation and the local Einstein relation. At finite Knudsen number, nonlocality produces intrinsic boundary slip and distinct size-scaling behavior of the drift mobility $μ$ and hydrodynamic thermal conductivity $κ_H(y)$. First-principles calculations for graphite show that nonlocality broadens the channel-size window over which thermal conductivity exhibits superlinear size dependence. Temperature-dependent simulations further show that the nonlocal theory captures the emergence of a hydrodynamic peak in thermal conductivity normalized by ballistic conductance, whereas the local theory fails to accurately predict its magnitude. Our results establish a microscopic connection between collective phonon mobility and heat conduction beyond local hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [145] [Temperature-dependent magnetic properties of the antiferromagnet Mn$_2$Au](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35153)
*Jackson L. Ross,Sarah Jenkins,Ricardco Rama-Eiroa,Paul-Iulian Gavriloaea,Jack Collins,Unai Atxitia,Oksana Chubykalo-Fesenko,Karin Everschor-Sitte,Rubén M. Otxoa,Roy W. Chantrell,Richard F. L. Evans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过原子自旋动力学模拟研究Mn₂Au单晶磁学性质的温度依赖性，建立了基于前期从头算的自旋哈密顿量模型，成功复现了各向异性常数和交换刚度的标度定律，并准确预测了畴壁宽度随温度的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₂Au是反铁磁自旋电子学中最有前景的材料之一，但要利用超短电/光手段调控其磁有序以实现超快应用，必须准确理解其磁学性质如何随温度变化。

Method: 采用原子自旋动力学模拟，基于前期从头算计算结果构建自旋哈密顿量，模拟Mn₂Au单晶在温度变化下的宏观磁学性质。

Result: 模型复现了各向异性常数随磁化强度呈Zener-Akulov-Callen-Callen幂律标度的行为；交换刚度随温度遵循磁化强度的m¹·⁸³标度定律；据此预测的畴壁宽度与直接模拟结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示了Mn₂Au磁学性质的温度依赖机制，为自旋电子学器件工作条件下的大规模模拟提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Mn$_2$Au is one of the most promising materials for antiferromagnetic spintronics due to its high Néel temperature, moderate anisotropy, layered spin structure allowing current induced spin-orbit-torques, and ultrafast spin dynamics. Further understanding and control of the magnetic order in Mn$_2$Au for ultrafast spintronics applications via ultrashort electric/optical means requires accurate understanding of how its magnetic properties depend on temperature. Here we present atomistic spin dynamics simulations of the temperature-dependent macroscopic magnetic properties of Mn$_2$Au single crystals. We introduce a spin Hamiltonian based on previous ab initio calculations which produces temperature-dependent scaling of the anisotropy constants with magnetisation consistent with Zener-Akulov-Callen-Callen power laws. The calculated temperature dependence of exchange stiffness follows a scaling law with magnetisation of $m^{1.83}_e$ , and predicts a temperature-dependent domain wall width that agrees very well with direct simulation. Our model provides an understanding of temperature-dependent magnetic properties of Mn$_2$Au that enables large-scale simulation of the working conditions of spintronic devices.

</details>


### [146] [Optical Thermometry in Monolayer WS2: Raman and Photoluminescence Spectroscopy from 5 K to 1273 K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35214)
*Gyeongjun Lee,Antoine Borel,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Fausto Sirotti,Fabian Cadiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立了一套覆盖5 K至1273 K的单层WS2光学测温框架，利用拉曼A1g模的线性温移和光致发光中中性激子发射的连续红移两种独立指标，实现宽温区、无接触式温度测量，并可推广到其他二维半导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠测温对二维半导体器件的表征与工程化至关重要，但现有二维材料光学测温只局限于数百开尔文的窄温度窗口，亟需建立覆盖更宽温度范围的测温方法。

Method: 作者针对单层WS2，在5–1273 K范围内综合运用两种光学指标：(i) 测量面外A1g拉曼模的频率随温度变化，分别对非封装样品（298–1223 K拟合）和hBN封装样品（100–1273 K拟合）拟合线性温移斜率；(ii) 在同一温度区间测量光致发光光谱，并采用半经验Pässler模型描述中性激子发射随温度的连续红移。

Result: 结果表明：(1) A1g模在100 K以上随温度线性移动，非封装WS2斜率为-0.0138 cm^-1/K，hBN封装样品斜率明显更小，为-0.0121 cm^-1/K；(2) 中性激子发射随温度持续红移，且Pässler模型拟合良好。两种指标分别反映晶格非谐性和带隙重整化两个不同物理机制。

Conclusion: 拉曼与光致发光两种指标物理机制不同、相互独立，共同实现了单层WS2在5–1273 K范围内的无接触测温，该方法可广泛推广至其他二维半导体材料。

Abstract: Reliable temperature measurement is essential for characterizing and engineering two-dimensional (2D) semiconductor devices, yet optical thermometry of 2D materials has so far been demonstrated only over narrow temperature windows spanning a few hundred kelvin. Here, we establish a comprehensive optical thermometry framework for monolayer WS2 from 5~K to 1273~K. We show that: (i) the out-of-plane A1g Raman mode shifts linearly with temperature above 100~K, with a slope of -0.0138 cm^{-1}/K in non-encapsulated WS2 (fitted over 298-1223~K) and a measurably smaller slope of -0.0121 cm^{-1}/K in an hBN-encapsulated sample (fitted over 100-1273~K); (ii) photoluminescence spectra measured over the same 5--1273~K range provide an independent optical indicator of the temperature: the neutral-exciton emission redshifts continuously with temperature and is well described by the semi-empirical Pässler model. The two indicators probe distinct physical mechanisms, lattice anharmonicity and bandgap renormalization, and together provide contact-free thermometry for monolayer WS2 from 5~K to 1273~K, an approach applicable to 2D semiconductors more broadly.

</details>


### [147] [Control of the Electronic and Atomic Structure of the Altermagnetic α-MnTe Surface via Substrate-Induced Surfactants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35236)
*Martin Heinrich,Mahdi Hajlaoui,Takuya Takashiro,Tetiana Zakusylo,Petr Babor,Ondrej Caha,Dawid Wutke,Natalia Olszowska,Gunther Springholz,Matthias Muntwiler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了反铁磁材料α-MnTe薄膜的表面原子结构如何随衬底不同而变化，以及这种变化对电子能带结构和自旋极化表面态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体（altermagnets）因其动量相关的自旋极化能带结构而具有自旋电子学应用潜力，其中α-MnTe因巨大的自旋劈裂和室温反铁磁序而备受关注。然而薄膜生长受衬底相互作用制约，表面原子结构差异会强烈影响表面电子态，因此需要厘清表面结构的控制与表征方法。

Method: 作者在两种不同衬底（SrF2(111) 与 InP(111)A）上生长α-MnTe薄膜，并利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）和扫描隧道谱（STS）等手段系统表征表面原子结构与电子能带结构。

Result: 研究发现：在SrF2(111)上形成原子级清洁、完美的(2x2)重构表面；而在InP(111)A上，由于In从衬底向表面的超偏析，MnTe表面始终被InTe层终止。这一InTe表面活性层呈现准周期位错网络以适配InTe/MnTe的晶格失配，并导致随表面终止方式不同而产生截然不同的表面态，显著改变表面电子能带结构。

Conclusion: 表面原子结构的控制与表征对理解反铁磁体性质至关重要，并为进一步在真实器件中工程化和调控自旋极化态提供了可能。

Abstract: Altermagnets are a novel class of materials with unique momentum dependent spin-polarization of the electronic band structure, which opens up new avenues for spintronic devices. Altermagnetic α-MnTe is particularly interesting for such applications due to its giant spin splitting and the persistence of altermagnetic order up to room temperature. Growth of thin films is, however, challenging and governed by interactions with the substrate. As a result, the surface of α-MnTe thin films exhibits very different atomic structures in dependence of the substrate material. Whereas on SrF2(111) an atomically clean, perfectly (2x2) reconstructed surface forms, on InP(111)A the MnTe surface is always InTe terminated due to super-segregation of In from the substrate to the surface. Most strikingly, the InTe surfactant layer displays a quasi-periodic dislocation network to accommodate the InTe/MnTe lattice mismatch. This leads to the emergence of distinct surface states in dependence of surface termination that strongly affect the electronic band structure at the surface as shown by angle resolved photoemission and tunneling spectroscopy. Thus, control and characterization of the atomic structure of the surface is fundamentally important for understanding the properties of altermagnets and provides room for engineering and tailoring of spin-polarized states in actual devices.

</details>


### [148] [Flexural Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35238)
*Songbo Li,Jinxiong Jia,Zeyu Li,Xudong Zhu,Jiaqi An,Feitong Chu,Songbo Zhang,Wenguang Zhu,Hualing Zeng,Zhenhua Qiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过弯曲形变在二维共线反铁磁体中实现与调控交变磁性（altermagnetism），并以单层MnPS3为例验证了d波与i波交变磁态及可切换的横向自旋流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性作为一种新兴磁性相，在自旋电子学中具有重要应用潜力，但如何灵活地工程化调控交变磁态仍是一个挑战。作者希望找到一种通用且可控的手段来在二维共线反铁磁体中实现并调控交变磁性。

Method: 作者以弯曲形变（flexural deformation）作为调控手段，通过设计不同的弯曲方式选择性地打破二维材料的不同对称性，从而诱导出具有不同波对称性（如d波、i波）的交变磁态；并利用紧束缚模型与第一性原理计算对单层MnPS3进行验证。

Result: 通过特定的弯曲形变，成功在单层MnPS3中实现d波与i波交变磁态，并能够反转自旋劈裂；在d波态中还实现了可切换的横向自旋电流。

Conclusion: 弯曲形变是一种多用途、可调控的途径，可用于在二维共线反铁磁体中设计交变磁性，为基于交变磁性的自旋电子器件提供了新的调控思路。

Abstract: We establish flexural deformation as a versatile route for engineering altermagnetism in two-dimensional collinear antiferromagnets. By tailoring the flexure, we selectively break different symmetries to realize altermagnetic states with distinct wave symmetries and reverse their spin splitting. A tight-binding model and first-principles calculations for monolayer MnPS3 demonstrate d- and i-wave states and a switchable transverse spin current in the d-wave state.

</details>


### [149] [Impact of organic spacer cations on the light and thermal stability of two-dimensional lead halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35247)
*Mahesh Eledath-Changarath,Jesús Ortiga-Fibla,Andrés F. Gualdrón-Reyes,Jesús Rodríguez-Romero,Iván Mora-Seró,Ana Cros,Núria Garro,Rodolfo Canet-Albiach,Juan P. Martínez-Pastor,Andrii Boichuk,Tetiana Boichuk,Juan F. Sánchez-Royo,Marie Krecmarova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用共聚焦微拉曼光谱和X射线光电子能谱技术，对比研究了BA2PbI4与PEA2PbI4两种二维铅卤钙钛矿在环境光与热应力下的稳定性，发现PEA有机间隔阳离子可改善晶体形貌、抑制晶格缺陷，从而延缓降解并增强抗光热能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期稳定性是制约二维铅卤钙钛矿在光伏和光电器件中应用的关键挑战，而有机间隔阳离子的选择不仅影响环境稳定性，还决定材料的结构特征和载流子动力学。

Method: 采用共聚焦微拉曼光谱和X射线光电子能谱技术，对新剥离的钙钛矿纳米片进行环境光稳定性与热稳定性分析，对比n-丁基铵（BA）和2-苯乙基铵（PEA）两种有机间隔阳离子体系。

Result: 两种材料室温发光性能相当，且均通过有机组分流失及快速晶相分解为PbI2和Pb0的方式降解；但PEA阳离子显著改善晶体形貌并抑制晶格缺陷，使降解更慢，对环境和光热应力具有更强抵抗力。

Conclusion: 结构和化学工程以及表面钝化和封装策略对实现二维卤化物钙钛矿器件的长期稳定性至关重要；同时，在稳定性与器件性能之间取得平衡仍是需要重点考虑的问题。

Abstract: Long-term stability remains a critical challenge limiting the photovoltaic and optoelectronic applications of two-dimensional lead halide perovskites. In these materials, the choice of the organic spacer plays a key role not only in enhancing environmental stability, but also in determining their structural characteristics and carrier dynamics. Here, we present an environmental photo- and thermal-stability analysis of BA2PbI4 and PEA2PbI4 two-dimensional lead halide perovskites by means of confocal micro-Raman and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy techniques. We have investigated two different organic spacers, n-butylammonium (BA) and 2-phenylethylammonium (PEA), which provide comparable room-temperature luminescence in freshly exfoliated perovskite nanoflakes and degrade similarly through the removal of organic components and fast crystal phase decomposition into PbI2 and Pb0. However, we observed that the PEA organic cation significantly improves crystal morphology and suppresses lattice defects, resulting in slower degradation and enhanced resistance to light and thermal stress under ambient conditions. Our findings indicate that structural and chemical engineering, along with surface passivation and encapsulation strategies, are essential for achieving long-term stability in two-dimensional halide perovskite devices. Nevertheless, optimizing the balance between stability and device performance remains a critical consideration.

</details>


### [150] [Investigation of the Microstructural and Magnetic Properties of Ni on GeSn Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35273)
*Tharundev V,Dushyant Singh,Subha Maity,Vigneshwari S R,Dhammapriy Gayakwad,Shivesh Yadav,Krista R Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究探究了锗锡(GeSn)晶体质量对Ni/GeSn界面演变及Ni层磁性响应的影响，通过在573 K和973 K快速热退火并借助XRD、XPS深度剖析和磁测量，发现GeSn结晶质量越差（Sn含量越高、退火温度越高），Ni-Ge-Sn互扩散与Sn表面偏析越严重，最终导致Ni层铁磁性被抑制、出现超顺磁并降低饱和磁化强度和矫顽力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镍层在锗锡上的热退火可在有限热预算内显著改变Ni薄膜及Ni/GeSn界面，但GeSn晶体质量如何影响Ni-Ge-Sn界面演变以及Ni层的磁响应尚不完全清楚，因此需要系统阐明结晶质量、界面扩散与磁行为之间的关联。

Method: 对Ni(15 nm)/Ge1-xSnx/Ge/Gd2O3/Si(111)异质结构（x=0.08和0.10）进行573 K和973 K、持续10 s的快速热退火，采用X射线衍射、极图分析、X射线光电子能谱(XPS)深度剖析以及温度依赖和磁场依赖的磁化强度测量进行表征，并以973 K作为严格的高温上限热参考。

Result: 573 K退火可保持Ge0.92Sn0.08的晶体织构，而Ge0.90Sn0.10出现微孪晶并结构完整性下降；973 K退火使两者均发生严重结构退化，高Sn样品退化更显著。XPS深度剖析显示，随退火温度升高存在渐进的Ni-Ge-Sn互混及明显的Sn表面偏析。磁测量表明，沉积态和573 K样品共存铁磁与超顺磁贡献，而973 K退火强烈抑制铁磁响应，产生高度不饱和的M(H)回线且饱和磁化强度和矫顽力降低，这与界面合金化和金属Ni层被稀释一致。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了GeSn晶体质量、界面扩散与磁行为之间的直接相关关系：GeSn结晶质量越差，界面扩散越剧烈，Ni层磁性退化越严重，表明通过控制GeSn结晶质量与退火热预算可调控Ni/GeSn界面的磁性能。

Abstract: Thermal processing of nickel (Ni) layers on germanium-tin (GeSn) can strongly modify the Ni film and the Ni/GeSn interface within a restricted thermal budget; however, the influence of GeSn crystalline quality on Ni-Ge-Sn interfacial evolution and the magnetic response of the Ni layer remains incompletely understood. Here, Ni (15~nm)/Ge$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$/Ge/Gd$_2$O$_3$/Si(111) heterostructures ($x = 0.08$ and $0.10$) were investigated following rapid thermal annealing at 573 and 973~K for 10~s using X-ray diffraction, pole-figure analysis, X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy depth profiling, and temperature- and field-dependent magnetization measurements. Annealing at 573~K preserves the crystallographic texture of Ge$_{0.92}$Sn$_{0.08}$, whereas Ge$_{0.90}$Sn$_{0.10}$ exhibits microtwin formation and reduced structural integrity. Annealing at 973~K, employed as a severe upper-bound thermal reference, causes extensive structural degradation in both heterostructures, with substantially greater deterioration in the higher-Sn sample. XPS depth profiling reveals progressive Ni-Ge-Sn intermixing accompanied by pronounced Sn surface segregation with increasing annealing temperature. Magnetization measurements show coexisting ferromagnetic and superparamagnetic contributions in the as-deposited and 573~K samples, whereas annealing at 973~K strongly suppresses the ferromagnetic response, producing highly unsaturated $M(H)$ loops together with reduced saturation magnetization and coercivity. These magnetic changes are consistent with extensive interfacial alloying and dilution of the metallic Ni layer. This study establishes a direct correlation between GeSn crystalline quality, interfacial diffusion, and magnetic behavior.

</details>


### [151] [Simultaneous suppression and temperature-driven crossover of particle-like and wave-like thermal transport in trigonal CsAg$_3$S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35360)
*Vivek Gusain,B K Mani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过自洽声子理论（含三阶、四阶非谐性）与热输运统一理论，研究三方相 P-3m1 CsAg3S2 的热输运，预测其在 300 K 具有 0.222 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹ 的超低晶格热导率，并阐明了粒子性（κp）与波动性（κc）双通道同时被抑制的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在晶体材料中同时抑制粒子性（κp）与波动性（κc）热输运通道十分困难，因为削弱一个通道往往会增强另一个通道；作者旨在寻找能同时压低两者的材料与机制。

Method: 采用自洽声子理论（包含立方与四次非谐性）结合热输运统一理论计算三方相 P-3m1 CsAg3S2 的声子输运，并利用动量弛豫时间近似（MRTA）计算电输运性质，进而评估热电优值 ZT。

Result: 预测 300 K 时超低晶格热导率为 0.222 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，κp 与 κc 同时较低，且热导率呈约 T⁻⁰·⁴² 的弱温度依赖，显著偏离传统 T⁻¹ 行为。局域化的 Cs 模增强非谐散射、较软的 Ag-S 框架降低群速度，共同抑制 κp；稀疏且彼此分离的高频 S 衍生光学模则通过限制声子模间相干耦合来抑制 κc。主输运通道随温度发生转变：300 K 时 κp 贡献约 74%，700 K 时 κc 贡献约 63%。结合电输运计算，n 型掺杂在 700 K 时 ZT 达 1.28。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了三方相 CsAg3S2 超低热导率的微观机制，并指出在 AM3Ch2 三元硫属化合物家族中，共价 M-Ch 骨架的修饰可作为键层级设计原则下进一步降低 κp 的额外调控参数。

Abstract: The simultaneous suppression of particle-like ($κ_p$ ) and wave-like ($κ_c$) thermal transport channels in crystalline materials remains a challenging problem, as reducing one channel can enhance the other. Here, we investigate the thermal transport in trigonal $P\Bar{3}m1$ CsAg$_3$S$_2$ using self-consistent phonon theory with cubic and quartic anharmonicity and the unified theory of thermal transport.
  Our calculations predict an ultralow lattice thermal conductivity of 0.222 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$ at 300 K, featuring simultaneously low $κ_p$ and $κ_c$ with a weak temperature dependence of $\sim$T$^{-0.42}$, which deviates significantly from the conventional $\sim$T$^{-1}$ behavior. This simultaneous suppression arises from localized Cs modes, which enhance anharmonic scattering, and the softer Ag-S framework, which reduces phonon group velocities, together suppressing $κ_p$, in conjunction with sparse, well-separated high-frequency S-derived optical modes that suppress $κ_c$ by limiting the coherent coupling between the phonon modes. Furthermore, the dominant transport channel exhibits a temperature-dependent crossover, with $κ_p$ contributing $\sim$74 \% of total lattice thermal conductivity at 300~K and $κ_c$ contributing $\sim$63 \% at 700~K. Combining the ultralow thermal conductivity with electrical transport calculated within the momentum relaxation time approximation (MRTA), we obtain a $ZT$ of 1.28 for $n$-type doping at 700 K. The present work not only clarifies the microscopic mechanism of ultralow thermal conductivity in trigonal CsAg$_3$S$_2$ but also identifies the covalent M-Ch framework modification within the AM$_3$Ch$_2$ family of ternary chalcogenides as an additional tuning parameter for further reducing $κ_p$ within the bonding-hierarchy design principle.

</details>


### [152] [Zeeman-field-induced three-spin interactions and relativistic coupling to the scalar spin chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35401)
*Samir Lounis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文从微观电子哈密顿量出发证明，传导电子的塞曼耦合会在线性磁场下产生三类三体自旋相互作用：无需自旋轨道耦合的各向同性项、需自旋轨道耦合的相对论性项（包括与标量自旋手性耦合的 $\mathbf K_{ijk}\cdot\mathbf B\,\chi_{ijk}$），以及轨道通道恢复的莫特绝缘体轨道磁通机制。这些项能移动磁化台阶、改变场致非共面性，并在连续极限中劈裂斯格明子/反斯格明子、产生依赖磁场的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋模型中通常仅通过单个磁矩的塞曼能引入磁场，但传导电子塞曼耦合在磁场线性阶、时间反演对称允许的最低阶上可能产生被忽略的三体自旋相互作用，这些高阶项对磁化过程与拓扑自旋结构有潜在影响。

Method: 从微观电子哈密顿量出发，利用巨势的格林函数展开，识别出两类三体相互作用；通过 Hubbard 三聚体的精确对角化验证自旋通道与轨道通道的独立性和可加性；对 Rashba 电子气中的磁性三聚体作微扰展开得到解析表达式；最后在连续极限下分析手性耦合对自旋纹理的作用。

Result: 各向同性项 $(\mathbf S_i\cdot\mathbf S_j)(\mathbf S_k\cdot\mathbf B)$ 无需自旋轨道耦合即存在，其总强度满足涉及费米能处三位点电子环路的求和规则；相对论项需自旋轨道耦合，其中 $\mathbf K_{ijk}\cdot\mathbf B\,\chi_{ijk}$ 在无自旋轨道耦合的自旋通道中被禁戒，而轨道通道恢复莫特绝缘体的轨道磁通机制。对 Rashba 气体中的三聚体，$\mathbf K_{ijk}$ 在一阶自旋轨道耦合时位于面内，且在 $C_{3v}$ 对称三聚体中消失，二阶时出现面外分量。这些相互作用可移动磁化台阶场并改变场致非共面性；连续极限下手性耦合劈裂斯格明子与反斯格明子，相对论项产生依赖磁场的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用。

Conclusion: 传导电子塞曼耦合在磁场下产生超越单体塞曼能的三体自旋相互作用：各向同性项与标量手性耦合相对论项。后者在连续极限中劈裂斯格明子/反斯格明子并诱导场依赖的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用，且自旋与轨道通道独立可加，对理解磁化台阶、场致非共面性及拓扑磁结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are commonly incorporated in atomistic spin models through the Zeeman energy of individual magnetic moments. Starting from a microscopic electronic Hamiltonian, we show that the Zeeman coupling of conduction electrons also generates field-linear three-spin interactions, the lowest-order corrections allowed by time-reversal symmetry. Using a Green-function expansion of the grand potential, we identify two classes of interactions. Isotropic terms of the form $(\mathbf S_i\cdot\mathbf S_j)(\mathbf S_k\cdot\mathbf B)$ exist without spin-orbit coupling, and their total strength obeys a sum rule involving the three-site electronic loop at the Fermi energy. Relativistic terms require spin-orbit coupling and include $\mathbf K_{ijk}\cdot\mathbf B,χ_{ijk}$, where $χ_{ijk}=\mathbf S_i\cdot(\mathbf S_j\times\mathbf S_k)$ is the scalar spin chirality. We show that this coupling is forbidden in the spin channel without spin-orbit coupling, whereas the orbital channel recovers the orbital-flux mechanism of Mott insulators. Exact diagonalization of the Hubbard trimer confirms the independence and additivity of both channels. For magnetic trimers in Rashba electron gases, we derive analytical expressions showing that $\mathbf K_{ijk}$ lies in-plane at first order in spin-orbit coupling but vanishes for $C_{3v}$-symmetric trimers, where an out-of-plane component emerges at second order. These interactions can shift magnetization-step fields and modify field-induced noncoplanarity. In the continuum limit, the chirality coupling splits skyrmions from antiskyrmions, while the relativistic terms generate a field-dependent Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction.

</details>


### [153] [Pulsed Laser Deposition of Ferroelectric SrTiO3 Shells Enables Polarization-Controlled Charge Separation in TiO2 Photoanode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35454)
*Yiqi Jin,Weizeng Zheng,Beibei Wang,Lei Zhao,Binmin Wu,Leonhard Mayrhofer,Xiangjian Meng,Hao Shen,Xiaodan Wang,Weiguang Huang,Junhao Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用脉冲激光沉积在TiO₂纳米棒阵列上制备原子级定义的SrTiO₃铁电壳层，发现光电流密度随壳层厚度呈火山形依赖关系，峰值达1.9 mA cm⁻²（1.23 V vs RHE），并证实相干界面应变（而非缺陷偶极子）是铁电有序的来源，建立了"应变-极化-内建电场-电荷分离"的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电极化是调控光电极界面电荷分离的有力途径，但传统化学转化的铁电壳层存在亚纳米厚度不可控、相干应变无法保留、化学界面不洁净等缺陷，限制了其从经验优化走向原子级可控的预测性设计。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积在热水热生长的TiO₂纳米棒阵列上外延生长化学计量比的SrTiO₃壳层，构建原子级定义的核壳结构并保持一维输运几何；通过控制沉积时间（如优化的15分钟样品）调控壳层厚度；利用高分辨透射电镜、X射线光电子能谱、开尔文探针力显微镜及器件级光电化学测试进行结构、化学、静电和性能的多维度表征。

Result: 光电流密度随壳层厚度呈火山形变化，15分钟优化样品在AM 1.5G、1.23 V vs RHE下达到1.9 mA cm⁻²，超过热水热基准。高分辨电镜和XPS表明壳层为四方畸变且氧化学计量比完整，确认相干界面应变而非缺陷偶极子是铁电序的起源；开尔文探针力显微镜直接成像了极化诱导的内建电场，其强度与电荷转移效率和光电流密度正相关。

Conclusion: 通过互补的结构、化学、静电与器件级测量，建立了定量的"应变-极化-内建电场-电荷分离"关系，将铁电光阳极工程从经验优化转变为原子级可控、可预测的材料策略，并可拓展至可见光吸收材料和复杂铁电异质结构，用于高效太阳能分解水器件。

Abstract: Ferroelectric polarization offers a powerful route to govern the interfacial charge separation in photoelectrodes; however, chemically converted ferroelectric shells lack sub-nanometer thickness control, coherent strain retention, and chemically clean interfaces. Here, we use pulsed laser deposition to fabricate stoichiometric SrTiO3 shells on hydrothermally grown TiO2 nanorod arrays, achieving atomically defined core-shell architectures while preserving the one-dimensional transport geometry. The photocurrent density followed a volcano-shaped dependence on shell thickness and reached 1.9 mA cm-2 at 1.23 V versus RHE under AM 1.5G illumination for the optimized 15-mins sample, exceeding the established hydrothermal benchmark. High-resolution transmission electron microscopy and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy revealed a tetragonally distorted, oxygen-stoichiometric shell, identifying coherent interfacial strain rather than defect dipoles as the origin of the ferroelectric order. Kelvin probe force microscopy directly imaged a polarization-induced built-in electric field whose magnitude scaled with charge-transfer efficiency and photocurrent density. Together, these complementary structural, chemical, electrostatic, and device-level measurements established a quantitative strain-polarization-built-in field-charge separation relationship. This work transforms ferroelectric photoanode engineering from empirical optimization into an atomically controllable and predictive materials strategy for high-efficiency solar water-splitting devices. This approach is readily extendable to visible-light absorbers and complex ferroelectric heterostructures.

</details>


### [154] [Multi-objective Bayesian optimization reveals HiPIMS deposition rates exceeding direct current sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35535)
*Nathan Rodkey,Felix Thelen,Alfred Ludwig,Sebastian Siol*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用多目标贝叶斯优化高效探索HiPIMS脉冲包过程空间，发现含单脉冲在内的多种HiPIMS过程沉积速率可超过DC参考值，挑战了薄膜制造中"质量-速度"长期权衡的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiPIMS虽具备高离化通量分数等独特优势，但其离化离子受到负偏压靶的回吸作用，导致部分过程沉积速率偏低。为克服这一问题而提出的双极、多脉冲、斩波等复杂脉冲方案又带来了过程复杂性的问题——过程空间可膨胀至1E5-1E20种组合，亟需高效智能的采样方法。

Method: 采用多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）对脉冲包（pulse-packets）过程空间进行高效采样，以高离化通量分数和高沉积速率为优化目标，探索过程中采样了超过2000个不同过程。

Result: 在1.0 Pa工作气压和5-20%中等离化率条件下，毫秒级脉冲表现最优；令人意外的是，包括单脉冲在内的许多HiPIMS过程沉积速率可超过DC参考值。作者推测这些过程最大化了气体稀薄化效应，在靶与基片之间形成低碰撞通道。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了脉冲溅射过程先前未被报道的行为，挑战了薄膜制造中薄膜质量与沉积速度之间长期存在的权衡观点，为HiPIMS工艺设计与优化开辟了新思路。

Abstract: Pulsed sputter processes are essential for a wide range of applications from hard coatings to microelectronics. High power impulse magnetron sputtering (HiPIMS) offers unique possibilities for process design due to its high ionized flux fraction. Because of the high peak-current densities involved, correspondingly high plasma densities generate many ions. This enables tuning of kinetic energies by applying accelerating bias potentials. However, some HiPIMS processes have low deposition rates due to the back-attraction these ions experience with the negatively charged sputter target. Recent work in HiPIMS has targeted this problem, exploring complex pulsing schemes such as bipolar, multi-pulse, or chopped HiPIMS. These pulsing schemes introduce another problem: process complexity. As such, the process space, even with coarse intervals quickly blows up to 1E5-1E20 possible process combinations. This calls for efficient and intelligent sampling, which can be enabled by machine-learning algorithms such as Bayesian optimization. In this work we use multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) to efficiently sample the process space of pulse-packets, targeting high ionization flux fractions and deposition rates. Over 2,000 distinct processes were sampled during this exploration. Surprisingly, we found that many HiPIMS processes, including single pulse, can achieve deposition rates higher than DC references. In fact, at a working pressure of 1.0 Pa and moderate ionization rates of 5-20% we found that ms-scale pulses were ideal. We hypothesize these processes maximize gas rarefaction, creating a low-collision channel between the target and the substrate. These results showcase previously unreported behavior of pulsed sputter processes and challenge a long-held trade-off between film quality and speed in thin-film manufacturing.

</details>


### [155] [High-efficiency solid-state electrochemical thermal switching through phonon engineering of 2D materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35547)
*Harry Mclean,Steven Paul Hepplestone*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过机器学习原子间势与DFT筛选57种过渡金属二硫化物（TMDCs），发现Li插层的ZrS₂具有优异的可逆热开关性能：嵌锂后横向热导率从约3.7降至0.05 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，远超已报道的固态电化学热开关，并展示了在废热回收中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆且具有大热对比度的固态热开关对于先进热管理至关重要，但目前此类材料十分稀缺。作者旨在寻找能够通过电化学插层实现超大热导率切换的层状材料。

Method: 采用通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）与密度泛函理论（DFT）相结合，系统筛选57种过渡金属二硫化物，覆盖1T和2H两种相以及Li和Mg插层。通过计算热导率、热容和声子特性，筛选出Li_aZrS₂，并借助器件级建模评估其废热回收性能；同时分析Zr–Zr框架的软化（平均力常数由约4.2降至1.2 eV Å⁻²）和声学声子散射增强机制。

Result: 嵌锂至Li₀.₁₂₅ZrS₂使横向热导率由约3.7 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹降至0.05 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，切换性能超过此前报道的固态电化学热开关。器件级建模表明脉冲系统可捕获理想开关86%的回收能量。该切换行为归因于Zr–Zr框架显著软化以及传热声学声子的散射增强。

Conclusion: 电化学插层是调控层状材料热输运的一种有前景的途径，LiₐZrS₂结合了优异的热导率切换能力与低热容，为先进热管理和废热回收提供了新的材料平台。

Abstract: Reversible solid-state thermal switches with large thermal contrasts remain rare, yet are essential for advanced thermal management. Using universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) and density functional theory (DFT), we screen 57 transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs), spanning the 1T and 2H phases with Li and Mg intercalation. Among the candidates, Li$_a$ZrS$_2$ combines exceptional thermal conductivity switching with low heat capacity. Lithiation to Li$_{0.125}$ZrS$_2$ reduces the cross-plane thermal conductivity from approximately 3.7 to 0.05 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$. The switching behaviour exceeds that of previously reported solid-state electrochemical thermal switches. Device-level modelling further demonstrates the potential for waste-heat recovery, with a pulsed system capturing 86\% of the energy recovered by an ideal switch. We attribute the switching behaviour to substantial softening of the Zr--Zr framework, with the mean force constant decreasing from approximately 4.2 to 1.2 eV $\mathrm{\mathring{A}}^{-2}$, alongside enhanced scattering of heat-carrying acoustic phonons. These findings establish electrochemical intercalation as a promising route to engineering thermal transport in layered materials.

</details>


### [156] [Retention-constrained fine-tuning of a pre-trained aluminium MACE potential for non-equilibrium defect dynamics and plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35653)
*Yue Wu,Yuan Tian,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保留约束的微调协议，用于在提升铝 MACE 势 QP17 对非平衡缺陷动力学与塑性描述精度的同时，保持其近平衡弹性和振动响应不退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练的机器学习原子间势用于非平衡缺陷动力学与塑性时，需要在提升缺陷精度的同时不牺牲近平衡弹性与振动响应，二者往往相互冲突，亟需一种能兼顾的微调策略。

Method: 以铝 MACE 势 QP17 为对象，整合条件读出不确定性（基于 144 个线性读出权重的敏感性引导采集）、迭代全参数更新与协议兼容的相对能量锚定，并在批内进行协方差更新与神经表征演化。经过四轮主动学习（32 个采集构型、400 次梯度更新）后，与随机采样、几何多样性选择、全标签均匀训练三种基线在相同更新预算下对比。

Result: 力不确定性采集将平均开发力误差从 22.43 降至 21.52 meV/Å，优于随机采样（22.70）、几何多样性选择（22.46）和全标签均匀训练（22.71）。所有力不确定性运行均满足体相力学保留标准：历史相对能量误差从 0.375 降至 0.207 meV/原子，谐波声子均方根漂移保持在 0.05 THz 以下。匹配目标消融表明相对能量锚定抑制了谐波漂移。在平面层错、孪晶界和晶界的样本外评估中，缺陷能量与偏应力获得可迁移增益，尽管从未拟合维里应力。MD-DFT 闭环试点证实自动验收门控能拦截违反历史弹性组能量或振动容差的更新。

Conclusion: 上述组件共同建立了一种保留约束的微调协议，能在改善塑性变形可观测量的同时，保留经过测试的体相力学响应，为机器学习原子间势向非平衡缺陷场景的稳健迁移提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Adapting a pre-trained machine-learning interatomic potential for non-equilibrium defect dynamics and plasticity requires improving defect accuracy without degrading the near-equilibrium elastic and vibrational response. Here we achieve this balance for the aluminium MACE potential QP17 by integrating conditional readout uncertainty, iterative full-parameter updates and protocol-compatible relative-energy anchoring. Sensitivities over 144 linear readout weights guide acquisition, with within-batch covariance updates and evolving neural representations. After four active-learning rounds (32 acquired configurations and 400 gradient updates), force-uncertainty acquisition reduces mean development force error from 22.43 to 21.52 meV/Angstrom, outperforming random sampling (22.70), geometry-diversity selection (22.46) and uniform training across all 104 candidate labels (22.71) under the same update budget. All three force-uncertainty runs satisfy bulk-mechanical retention criteria: historical relative-energy error decreases from 0.375 to 0.207 meV/atom, while harmonic phonon root-mean-square drift remains below 0.05 THz. Matched objective ablations reveal that relative-energy anchoring suppresses harmonic drift. Out-of-sample evaluations across planar faults, twin boundaries and grain boundaries demonstrate transferable gains in defect energies and deviatoric stresses, even though virial stresses are never fitted. A closed-loop molecular dynamics and density functional theory (MD-DFT) pilot confirms that automated acceptance gating intercepts updates that violate historical elastic-group energy or vibrational tolerances. Together, these components establish a retention-constrained fine-tuning protocol for improving plastic-deformation observables while preserving the tested bulk mechanical response.

</details>


### [157] [Phonon-assisted shift-current from first-principles: application to AlN monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35662)
*Yuncheng Mao,Pierre Lechifflart,Elena Cannuccia,Claudio Attaccalite*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种第一性原理方法，将特殊位移技术与基于动力学贝里相位的实时模拟相结合，用于研究扩展体系中声子辅助的位移电流，并以六方氮化铝（h-AlN）单层为例揭示温度与声子过程对非线性光电流的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究非线性光电流（尤其是位移电流）的方法多局限于零温、晶格冻结的理想情形，难以刻画有限温度下声子散射与声子辅助电子跃迁对位移电流的贡献，因此亟需一种能够自然纳入温度效应与晶格涨落的真实描述框架。

Method: 作者将特殊位移（special displacement）技术与基于动力学贝里相（dynamical Berry phase）的实时（real-time）模拟相结合，直接在含时动力学中纳入声子散射和声子辅助电子跃迁，从而在有限温度下求解非线性光学响应。

Result: 将该方法应用于具有间接带隙且缺乏中心反演对称性的六方AlN（h-AlN）单层的位移电流响应，结果表明声子介导过程对位移电流的幅值与光谱特征具有显著影响，温度成为决定位移电流的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该方法能够真实地描述有限温度下层状材料中的非线性光电流，证实了声子辅助过程和温度在位移电流生成中的重要作用，并为可实验制备的不同厚度h-AlN层提供了可验证的理论预测。

Abstract: In this work, we present a first-principles approach to investigate phonon-assisted shift current in extended systems. Our method combines the special displacement technique with real-time simulations based on the dynamical Berry phase. Merging these two frameworks enables direct access to non-linear optical responses at finite temperatures, while naturally incorporating phonon scattering and phonon-assisted electronic transitions into the time-dependent dynamics. This allows us to capture temperature-dependent features and lattice fluctuations that are essential for a realistic description of non-linear photocurrent generation. As a representative example, we apply our approach to the shift-current response of a monolayer of hexagonal AlN (h-AlN). This system provides an ideal testing ground because it features an indirect band gap and lacks inversion symmetry. Moreover, h-AlN layers of different thicknesses have been fabricated and characterized in a variety of experimental settings, opening the concrete possibility of testing our predictions. Our results demonstrate the relevance of phonon-mediated processes and the importance of temperature in determining the magnitude and spectral features of the shift current in layered materials.

</details>


### [158] [Third harmonic generation in the transition metal trichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35803)
*Timothy Y. Chung,Vergil M. Schreiber,Randy M. Sterbentz,David P. Shelton,Eduardo Flores,Jose Ramon Ares,Isabel J. Ferrer,Joshua O. Island*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了过渡金属三硫化物(TMTs)这一层状2D材料家族(ZrS3、HfS3、TiS3、NbS3)在1560 nm飞秒激光非共振激发下的偏振依赖三次谐波产生(THG)响应，发现其具有显著的光学非线性各向异性，各向异性比最高可达约6.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMTs作为一类具有强各向异性电学和光学性质的层状2D材料，其非线性光学各向异性响应尚未被系统研究。作者旨在通过偏振分辨的三次谐波测量，探索并比较该材料家族的非线性光学各向异性，并阐明其微观起源。

Method: 采用1560 nm飞秒激光对剥离的TMT晶体进行非共振偏振依赖三次谐波产生(THG)测量，覆盖ZrS3、HfS3、TiS3和NbS3四种材料。根据单斜和三斜晶体预期的非线性对称性依赖关系，提取相对非线性极化率系数(如chi_11/chi_22)，并分析各向异性比随材料变化及厚度依赖行为。

Result: 所有研究的TMT晶体均表现出强烈的偏振THG各向异性，各向异性比chi_11/chi_22最高约6.5。各向异性比在ZrS3中最大，并沿材料家族依次减小；该趋势与三次谐波能量接近各材料强b偏振吸收边的程度相关联。三斜结构的NbS3在厚薄片中表现出显著各向异性，但在最薄薄片中各向异性明显减弱，作者将其归因于驱动三斜畸变的Peierls型Nb-Nb二聚化的部分弛豫。

Conclusion: TMT晶体家族展现出区别于其他2D材料的巨大各向异性非线性响应，使其成为进一步研究非线性光学现象的理想平台，尤其在偏振敏感光子和光电子应用方面具有吸引力。

Abstract: The transition metal trichalcogenides (TMTs) are an interesting class of layered 2D materials that have strong anisotropic electrical and optical properties. We report on the polarization-dependent nonlinear optical response of exfoliated crystals at the third harmonic using non-resonant excitation with a 1560 nm femtosecond laser. All members of the TMT crystal family explored (ZrS3, HfS3, TiS3, and NbS3) display large anisotropy in their polarized third harmonic generation signal, with anisotropy ratios as large as chi_11/chi_22 ~ 6.5. From the expected nonlinear dependence of these monoclinic and triclinic crystals we extract the relative nonlinear susceptibility coefficients. We find that the anisotropy ratio is largest for ZrS3 and decreases across the family, a trend we associate with the proximity of the third harmonic energy to the strongly b-polarized absorption edge of each material. NbS3, the only triclinic crystal, displays a pronounced anisotropy in thick flakes that is strongly reduced in the thinnest flakes, which we attribute to a partial relaxation of the Peierls-type Nb-Nb dimerization that underlies the triclinic distortion. The large anisotropic nonlinear response in this crystal family sets it apart from other 2D materials and makes it particularly attractive for further investigation.

</details>
